# Code Reviewer

Complete toolkit for code reviewer with modern tools and best practices.

## Quick Start

### Main Capabilities

This skill provides three core capabilities through automated scripts:

```bash
# Script 1: Pr Analyzer
python scripts/pr_analyzer.py [options]

# Script 2: Code Quality Checker
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py [options]

# Script 3: Review Report Generator
python scripts/review_report_generator.py [options]
```

## Core Capabilities

### 1. Pr Analyzer

Automated tool for pr analyzer tasks.

**Features:**
- Automated scaffolding
- Best practices built-in
- Configurable templates
- Quality checks

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/pr_analyzer.py <project-path> [options]
```

### 2. Code Quality Checker

Comprehensive analysis and optimization tool.

**Features:**
- Deep analysis
- Performance metrics
- Recommendations
- Automated fixes

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py <target-path> [--verbose]
```

### 3. Review Report Generator

Advanced tooling for specialized tasks.

**Features:**
- Expert-level automation
- Custom configurations
- Integration ready
- Production-grade output

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/review_report_generator.py [arguments] [options]
```

## Tech Stack

**Languages:** TypeScript, JavaScript, Python, Go, Swift, Kotlin
**Frontend:** React, Next.js, React Native, Flutter
**Backend:** Node.js, Express, GraphQL, REST APIs
**Database:** PostgreSQL, Prisma, NeonDB, Supabase
**DevOps:** Docker, Kubernetes, Terraform, GitHub Actions, CircleCI
**Cloud:** AWS, GCP, Azure

## Development Workflow

### 1. Setup and Configuration

```bash
# Install dependencies
npm install
# or
pip install -r requirements.txt

# Configure environment
cp .env.example .env
```

### 2. Run Quality Checks

```bash
# Use the analyzer script
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py .

# Review recommendations
# Apply fixes
```

### 3. Implement Best Practices

Follow the patterns and practices documented in:
- `references/code_review_checklist.md`
- `references/coding_standards.md`
- `references/common_antipatterns.md`

## Best Practices Summary

### Code Quality
- Follow established patterns
- Write comprehensive tests
- Document decisions
- Review regularly

### Performance
- Measure before optimizing
- Use appropriate caching
- Optimize critical paths
- Monitor in production

### Security
- Validate all inputs
- Use parameterized queries
- Implement proper authentication
- Keep dependencies updated

### Maintainability
- Write clear code
- Use consistent naming
- Add helpful comments
- Keep it simple

## Common Commands

```bash
# Development
npm run dev
npm run build
npm run test
npm run lint

# Analysis
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py .
python scripts/review_report_generator.py --analyze

# Deployment
docker build -t app:latest .
docker-compose up -d
kubectl apply -f k8s/
```

+    CORRECT_INFORMATION: "Correction d'informations",
+    CHANGE_DESTINATION: 'Changement de destination',
+};
+
+const DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
+    PENDING: { label: 'En attente', className: 'border border-ant-line text-ant-mid' },
+    APPROVED: { label: 'Approuvée', className: 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green' },
+    REJECTED: { label: 'Refusée', className: 'bg-ant-rose/10 text-ant-rose' },
+    UNKNOWN: { label: 'Mise à jour reçue', className: 'bg-ant-ink/5 text-ant-mid' },
+};
+
+/** Demand payload keys, in display order (written by the provider-integration edge fn). */
+const DEMAND_FIELDS: { key: string; label: string }[] = [
+    { key: 'phone', label: 'Téléphone' },
+    { key: 'receiver', label: 'Destinataire' },
+    { key: 'price', label: 'Prix' },
+    { key: 'note', label: 'Note' },
+    { key: 'city', label: 'Ville' },
+    { key: 'address', label: 'Adresse' },
+];
+
+const demandText = (v: unknown): string => {
+    if (v === null || v === undefined) return '';
+    if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'Oui' : 'Non';
+    if (typeof v === 'object') return '';
+    return String(v).trim();
+};
+
+/** Only the fields that actually changed — skip equal, both-empty, or absent-in-new. */
+const demandDiffs = (d: ChangeDemand) => {
+    const oldV = (d.old_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+    const newV = (d.new_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+    return DEMAND_FIELDS.flatMap(({ key, label }) => {
+        if (newV[key] === undefined) return [];
+        const next = demandText(newV[key]);
+        const prev = demandText(oldV[key]);
+        if (!next && !prev) return [];
+        // Numeric equality for the price only — a phone like 0612… must never be
+        // compared as a number (leading zero would make it equal to 612…).
+        const numeric = key === 'price' && prev !== '' && next !== ''
+            && Number.isFinite(Number(prev)) && Number.isFinite(Number(next));
+        if (numeric ? Number(prev) === Number(next) : prev === next) return [];
+        return [{ key, label, prev, next }];
+    });
+};
+
+const demandAuthor = (d: ChangeDemand): string => {
+    const u = d.created_by_user;
+    const row = Array.isArray(u) ? u[0] : u;
+    return (row?.name || '').trim();
+};
+

         // be treated as billing confirmations, not status changes. Drop "PAID" from the
         // status-mapping candidates so we don't error or wrongly downgrade the order.
         const dropPaid = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'PAID' ? null : v);

+        // ── Demand reconciliation (TKT-8b) ────────────────────────────────
+        // Coliaty documents NO webhook for parcel-change demands. Tolerantly
+        // treat any payload referencing a parcel_change_demands ref (PCD-...)
+        // — or an EVENT naming DEMAND/CHANGE_DEMAND even without a ref — as a
+        // reconciliation signal. Entirely fail-open: a bug here must never

+                // fallback, so free-text like "client accepted delivery" in a comment

+                        // never downgrade a demand already concluded APPROVED/REJECTED.

-- Parcel change demands (TKT-8 follow-up): local source of truth for the
-- Coliaty parcel-change demands WE file. Created by the provider-integration
-- edge fn (service role) at demand creation; reconciled fail-open by the
-- provider-webhook when a payload referencing the demand ref arrives.
-- Coliaty documents no demand webhook, so status/raw stay tolerant.

BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE public.parcel_change_demands (
    id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    "DigiCom Business" uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.businesses(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    order_id        uuid REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    provider_id     uuid REFERENCES public.providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    tracking_code   text NOT NULL,
    ref             text NOT NULL,
    request_type    text NOT NULL CHECK (request_type IN ('CORRECT_INFORMATION', 'CHANGE_DESTINATION')),
    -- PENDING at creation; APPROVED/REJECTED when a webhook lets us conclude;
    -- UNKNOWN when a webhook referenced the demand without a recognizable verdict.
    status          text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'UNKNOWN')),
    provider_status_raw text,
    -- Snapshots at file time: old = order/parcel values then, new = requested.
    old_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    new_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    raw             jsonb,
    created_by      uuid REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    resolved_at     timestamptz,
    webhook_log_id  uuid REFERENCES public.webhook_logs(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX parcel_change_demands_ref_key ON public.parcel_change_demands (ref);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_tracking_code ON public.parcel_change_demands (tracking_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_biz_created ON public.parcel_change_demands ("DigiCom Business", created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_order_id ON public.parcel_change_demands (order_id);

ALTER TABLE public.parcel_change_demands ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant read-only: members see their business's demands. All writes go through
-- the service role (edge fns) — no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy for users.
CREATE POLICY pcd_tenant_select ON public.parcel_change_demands
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id()));

GRANT SELECT ON public.parcel_change_demands TO authenticated;

COMMIT;

     delivery_service_statuses: string[] | null;
 };
 
+/** A parcel-change demand we filed with the provider (public.parcel_change_demands). */
+type ChangeDemand = {
+    id: string;
+    ref: string;
+    request_type: string;
+    status: string;
+    old_values: Record<string, unknown> | null;
+    new_values: Record<string, unknown> | null;
+    created_at: string;
+    resolved_at: string | null;
+    created_by_user?: { name?: string | null } | { name?: string | null }[] | null;
+};
+
+const DEMAND_TYPE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
+    CORRECT_INFORMATION: "Correction d'informations",
+    CHANGE_DESTINATION: 'Changement de destination',
+};
+
+const DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
+    PENDING: { label: 'En attente', className: 'border border-ant-line text-ant-mid' },
+    APPROVED: { label: 'Approuvée', className: 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green' },
+    REJECTED: { label: 'Refusée', className: 'bg-ant-rose/10 text-ant-rose' },
+    UNKNOWN: { label: 'Mise à jour reçue', className: 'bg-ant-ink/5 text-ant-mid' },
+};
+
+/** Demand payload keys, in display order (written by the provider-integration edge fn). */
+const DEMAND_FIELDS: { key: string; label: string }[] = [
+    { key: 'phone', label: 'Téléphone' },
+    { key: 'receiver', label: 'Destinataire' },
+    { key: 'price', label: 'Prix' },
+    { key: 'note', label: 'Note' },
+    { key: 'city', label: 'Ville' },
+    { key: 'address', label: 'Adresse' },
+];
+
+const demandText = (v: unknown): string => {
+    if (v === null || v === undefined) return '';
+    if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'Oui' : 'Non';
+    if (typeof v === 'object') return '';
+    return String(v).trim();
+};
+
+/** Only the fields that actually changed — skip equal, both-empty, or absent-in-new. */
+const demandDiffs = (d: ChangeDemand) => {
+    const oldV = (d.old_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+    const newV = (d.new_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+    return DEMAND_FIELDS.flatMap(({ key, label }) => {
+        if (newV[key] === undefined) return [];
+        const next = demandText(newV[key]);
+        const prev = demandText(oldV[key]);
+        if (!next && !prev) return [];
+        // Numeric equality for the price only — a phone like 0612… must never be
+        // compared as a number (leading zero would make it equal to 612…).
+        const numeric = key === 'price' && prev !== '' && next !== ''
+            && Number.isFinite(Number(prev)) && Number.isFinite(Number(next));
+        if (numeric ? Number(prev) === Number(next) : prev === next) return [];
+        return [{ key, label, prev, next }];
+    });
+};
+
+const demandAuthor = (d: ChangeDemand): string => {
+    const u = d.created_by_user;
+    const row = Array.isArray(u) ? u[0] : u;
+    return (row?.name || '').trim();
+};
+

     const queryClient = useQueryClient();

@@ -219,6 +219,102 @@ Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
         // be treated as billing confirmations, not status changes. Drop "PAID" from the
         // status-mapping candidates so we don't error or wrongly downgrade the order.
         const dropPaid = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'PAID' ? null : v);
+
+        // ── Demand reconciliation (TKT-8b) ────────────────────────────────
+        // Coliaty documents NO webhook for parcel-change demands. Tolerantly
+        // treat any payload referencing a parcel_change_demands ref (PCD-...)
+        // — or an EVENT naming DEMAND/CHANGE_DEMAND even without a ref — as a
+        // reconciliation signal. Entirely fail-open: a bug here must never
+        // break the normal status pipeline below.
+        try {
+            const payloadStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
+            // No trailing hyphen in the capture; refs normalized uppercase so the
+            // case-sensitive .eq('ref', …) below always matches the stored form.
+            const demandRefs = Array.from(new Set(
+                (payloadStr.match(/PCD-\d+(?:-\d+)*/gi) || []).map((r) => r.toUpperCase())
+            ));
+            const isDemandSignal = demandRefs.length > 0 || /DEMAND|CHANGE_DEMAND/.test(eventName);
+
+            if (isDemandSignal) {
+                // Verdict from STATUS-ish fields first; whole-payload scan only as a
+                // fallback, so free-text like "client accepted delivery" in a comment
+                // can't force APPROVED when a real status field is present.
+                const verdictSource = String(
+                    payload.DEMAND_STATUS ?? payload.STATUS ?? payload.STATUT ??
+                    payload.SITUATION ?? forcelogData?.status ?? ''
+                ).toLowerCase();
+                const scan = verdictSource || payloadStr.toLowerCase();
+                const verdict = /approuv|approved|accept/.test(scan)
+                    ? 'APPROVED'
+                    : /refus|reject|denied/.test(scan)
+                        ? 'REJECTED'
+                        : null;
+                const providerStatusRaw =
+                    payload.STATUS || payload.STATUT || payload.SITUATION ||
+                    forcelogData?.status || eventName || null;
+                const baseUpdate: any = {
+                    provider_status_raw: providerStatusRaw,
+                    resolved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+                    webhook_log_id: logEntry?.id ?? null,
+                };
+
+                for (const ref of demandRefs) {
+                    if (verdict) {
+                        // First conclusion wins: a replayed/late webhook must never flip
+                        // an already-concluded verdict (REJECTED→APPROVED or vice versa).
+                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
+                            .update({ ...baseUpdate, status: verdict })
+                            .eq('ref', ref)
+                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
+                            .in('status', ['PENDING', 'UNKNOWN']);
+                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
+                            .update(baseUpdate)
+                            .eq('ref', ref)
+                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
+                            .in('status', ['APPROVED', 'REJECTED']);
+                    } else {
+                        // No recognizable verdict: only mark UNKNOWN if still PENDING —
+                        // never downgrade a demand already concluded APPROVED/REJECTED.
+                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
+                            .update({ ...baseUpdate, status: 'UNKNOWN' })
+                            .eq('ref', ref)
+                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
+                            .eq('status', 'PENDING');
+                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
+                            .update(baseUpdate)
+                            .eq('ref', ref)
+                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
+                            .neq('status', 'PENDING');
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Reuse the same status-candidate extraction the normal pipeline
+                // builds below (~statusCandidates) to decide whether this payload
+                // ALSO carries a real status update alongside the demand signal.
+                const demandStatusCandidates = [
+                    payload.STATUT_S,

-- Parcel change demands (TKT-8 follow-up): local source of truth for the
-- Coliaty parcel-change demands WE file. Created by the provider-integration
-- edge fn (service role) at demand creation; reconciled fail-open by the
-- provider-webhook when a payload referencing the demand ref arrives.
-- Coliaty documents no demand webhook, so status/raw stay tolerant.

BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE public.parcel_change_demands (
    id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    "DigiCom Business" uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.businesses(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    order_id        uuid REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    provider_id     uuid REFERENCES public.providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    tracking_code   text NOT NULL,
    ref             text NOT NULL,
    request_type    text NOT NULL CHECK (request_type IN ('CORRECT_INFORMATION', 'CHANGE_DESTINATION')),
    -- PENDING at creation; APPROVED/REJECTED when a webhook lets us conclude;
    -- UNKNOWN when a webhook referenced the demand without a recognizable verdict.
    status          text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'UNKNOWN')),
    provider_status_raw text,
    -- Snapshots at file time: old = order/parcel values then, new = requested.
    old_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    new_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    raw             jsonb,
    created_by      uuid REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    resolved_at     timestamptz,
    webhook_log_id  uuid REFERENCES public.webhook_logs(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX parcel_change_demands_ref_key ON public.parcel_change_demands (ref);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_tracking_code ON public.parcel_change_demands (tracking_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_biz_created ON public.parcel_change_demands ("DigiCom Business", created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_order_id ON public.parcel_change_demands (order_id);

ALTER TABLE public.parcel_change_demands ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant read-only: members see their business's demands. All writes go through
-- the service role (edge fns) — no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy for users.
CREATE POLICY pcd_tenant_select ON public.parcel_change_demands
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id()));

GRANT SELECT ON public.parcel_change_demands TO authenticated;

COMMIT;

  3902	    async sessionFetch(path, method, formBody, _retried) {
  3903	        await this.ensureSession();
  3904	        const headers = {
  3905	            'Cookie': this.cookieHeader,
  3906	            'Accept': 'application/json',
  3907	            'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
  3908	            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': this.csrfToken,
  3909	            'Referer': `${this.apiBaseUrl}/client`,
  3910	        };
  3911	        const init = { method: method || 'GET', redirect: 'manual', headers };
  3912	        if (formBody) {
  3913	            const body = formBody instanceof URLSearchParams ? formBody : new URLSearchParams(formBody);
  3914	            if (!body.has('_token')) body.set('_token', this.csrfToken);
  3915	            headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded';
  3916	            init.body = body.toString();
  3917	        }
  3918	        const res = await fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, init);
  3919	        // Laravel rotates session cookies on every request — harvest them so the next call
  3920	        // doesn't 401. Without this, /post/ajax fails right after a successful /post/add.
  3921	        const fresh = this.parseSetCookies(res.headers);
  3922	        if (fresh) this.cookieHeader = this.mergeCookies(this.cookieHeader, fresh);
  3923	        // 401 → session expired. Re-login once.
  3924	        if (res.status === 401 && !_retried) {
  3925	            this.cookieHeader = null;
  3926	            this.csrfToken = null;
  3927	            return this.sessionFetch(path, method, formBody, true);
  3928	        }
  3929	        const text = await res.text();
  3930	        let data;
  3931	        try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch { return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data: null, raw: text }; }
  3932	        return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data };
  3933	    }

  3936	        await this.ensureSession();
  3937	        const res = await fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, {
  3938	            method: 'GET',
  3939	            redirect: 'manual',
  3940	            headers: { 'Cookie': this.cookieHeader, 'Accept': 'application/pdf, application/json' },
  3941	        });
  3942	        const fresh = this.parseSetCookies(res.headers);
  3943	        if (fresh) this.cookieHeader = this.mergeCookies(this.cookieHeader, fresh);
  3944	        if (!res.ok) {
  3945	            const t = await res.text();
  3946	            throw new Error(`Delevo PDF HTTP ${res.status}: ${t.substring(0, 200)}`);
  3947	        }
  3948	        const buf = await res.arrayBuffer();
  3949	        const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf);
  3950	        let bin = '';
  3951	        for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) bin += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
  3952	        return `data:application/pdf;base64,${btoa(bin)}`;

  4040	    async trackParcel(trackingCode, _externalId, supabase) {
  4041	        if (!trackingCode) throw new Error('Tracking code required');
  4042	        // 1. Try the public /api/client/colis/track/{code} first. As of 2026-04, Delvo's
  4043	        // own endpoint returns 500 for every parcel — but if they fix it later, we'll pick
  4044	        // up real audit events automatically.

  4046	        if (apiResp.ok && Array.isArray(apiResp.data?.data) && apiResp.data.data.length > 0) {
  4047	            const audits = [...apiResp.data.data].sort((a, b) => Number(a.Date_Evenement || 0) - Number(b.Date_Evenement || 0));
  4048	            const history = [];
  4049	            let last = null;
  4050	            for (const a of audits) {
  4051	                const s = a?.status;
  4052	                if (!s || s === last) continue;
  4053	                last = s;
  4054	                const master = await this.resolveMasterStatus(s, supabase);
  4055	                history.push({
  4056	                    status: s,
  4057	                    statusLabel: master?.label || s,
  4058	                    statusColor: master?.color || null,
  4059	                    statusId: master?.id || null,
  4060	                    color: master?.color || null,
  4061	                    date: a?.Date_Evenement ? new Date(Number(a.Date_Evenement) * 1000).toISOString() : '',
  4062	                    comment: '',
  4063	                });
  4064	            }
  4065	            const latest = history.length ? history[history.length - 1] : null;
  4066	            return {
  4067	                tracking_code: trackingCode,
  4068	                status: latest?.status || '',
  4069	                statusLabel: latest?.statusLabel || latest?.status || '',
  4070	                statusColor: latest?.statusColor || null,
  4071	                status_code: null,
  4072	                history,
  4073	            };
  4074	        }

  4343	// Shared caller-authorization guard for the two Coliaty parcel-change-demand
  4344	// actions (get_allowed_destination_cities / create_parcel_change_demand):
  4345	// resolves the bearer JWT to a user, then requires the target company_parcels
  4346	// row (matched by tracking_code + provider_id) to belong to the caller's own
  4347	// business ("DigiCom Business" on both users and company_parcels). Throws an
  4348	// Error with status_code (401/403) — the callers' existing status_code catch
  4349	// blocks already know how to turn that into a Response. Returns the resolved
  4350	// { userId, businessId, parcelOrderId } for callers that persist a demand row.
  4351	async function assertParcelChangeAccess(req, supabase, providerId, trackingCode) {
  4352	    const jwt = (req.headers.get('Authorization') ?? '').replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '').trim();
  4353	    if (!jwt) {
  4354	        const err: any = new Error('Authentification requise.');
  4355	        err.status_code = 401;
  4356	        throw err;
  4357	    }
  4358	    const { data: { user }, error: userErr } = await supabase.auth.getUser(jwt);
  4359	    if (userErr || !user) {
  4360	        const err: any = new Error('Authentification requise.');
  4361	        err.status_code = 401;
  4362	        throw err;
  4363	    }
  4364	    const { data: me } = await supabase
  4365	        .from('users')
  4366	        .select('"DigiCom Business"')
  4367	        .eq('id', user.id)
  4368	        .maybeSingle();
  4369	    const { data: parcel } = await supabase
  4370	        .from('company_parcels')
  4371	        .select('"DigiCom Business", order_id')
  4372	        .eq('provider_id', providerId)
  4373	        .eq('tracking_code', trackingCode)
  4374	        .maybeSingle();
  4375	    const callerBiz = me?.['DigiCom Business'];
  4376	    const parcelBiz = parcel?.['DigiCom Business'];
  4377	    if (!callerBiz || !parcelBiz || callerBiz !== parcelBiz) {
  4378	        const err: any = new Error('Accès refusé pour ce colis.');
  4379	        err.status_code = 403;
  4380	        throw err;
  4381	    }
  4382	    return { userId: user.id, businessId: callerBiz, parcelOrderId: parcel?.order_id ?? null };
  4383	}

  4603	                        }
