  4618	                        if (pcdErr) console.error('parcel_change_demands upsert failed:', pcdErr);
  4619	                    } catch (persistErr) {
  4620	                        console.error('parcel_change_demands persistence failed:', persistErr);
  4621	                    }
  4622	                } catch (err: any) {
  4623	                    if (typeof err?.status_code === 'number') {
  4624	                        return new Response(JSON.stringify({
  4625	                            error: err.message,
  4626	                            status_code: err.status_code,
  4627	                            ...(err.errors ? { errors: err.errors } : {})
  4628	                        }), {
  4629	                            status: err.status_code,
  4630	                            headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  4631	                        });
  4632	                    }
  4633	                    throw err;
  4634	                }
  4635	                break;
     1	// @ts-nocheck - Deno runtime types not recognized by VSCode
     2	import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
     3	import { createClient } from 'jsr:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
     4	
     5	const corsHeaders = {
     6	    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     7	    'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     8	};
     9	
    10	/* Status classification now lives on the master row as semantic flags:
    11	 *   - flow_rank (smallint): forward-progression order (new=0 … out_for_delivery=4, NULL otherwise);
    12	 *     the webhook must never move an order to a LOWER flow_rank.
    13	 *   - is_terminal: delivered/returned/refused/cancelled — once terminal, only same-class updates
    14	 *     are allowed (prevents a stale push from clobbering a finished order; exchange delivered→returned
    15	 *     is the one sanctioned cross-class transition).
    16	 *   - is_delivered / is_returned / is_cancelled / is_postponed / is_new: side-effect + class flags.
    17	 * No category strings are used here. */
    18	
    19	/* Parse Coliaty DATE field. Coliaty's API reference shows two formats in production:
    20	 *   - PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED:    "20/06/2025"  (dd/mm/yyyy)
    21	 *   - PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED: "14-05-2026"  (dd-mm-yyyy)
    22	 * Returns an ISO string anchored at 12:00 UTC of that day (avoids day-boundary drift on
    23	 * timezone conversion) or null when the input is missing or unparseable. */
    24	function parseColiatyDate(raw: unknown): string | null {
    25	    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
    26	    const trimmed = raw.trim();
    27	    const match = trimmed.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{4})$/);
    28	    if (!match) return null;
    29	    const day = parseInt(match[1], 10);
    30	    const month = parseInt(match[2], 10);
    31	    const year = parseInt(match[3], 10);
    32	    if (day < 1 || day > 31 || month < 1 || month > 12 || year < 2000) return null;
    33	    const utc = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, 12, 0, 0);
    34	    if (Number.isNaN(utc)) return null;
    35	    return new Date(utc).toISOString();
    36	}
    37	
    38	Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
    39	    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    40	        return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    const json = (body: object, status = 200) =>
    44	        new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    45	            status,
    46	            headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    47	        });
    48	
    49	    try {
    50	        // ── 1. Extract provider ID + secret from URL ────────────────────
    51	        const url = new URL(req.url);
    52	        const pathParts = url.pathname.split('/');
    53	        const webhookSecret = pathParts[pathParts.length - 1];
    54	        const providerId = pathParts[pathParts.length - 2];
    55	
    56	        if (!providerId || !webhookSecret) {
    57	            return json({ error: 'Invalid URL' }, 400);
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        // ── 2. Init Supabase (service-role) ─────────────────────────────
    61	        const supabase = createClient(
    62	            Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    63	            Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    64	        );
    65	
    66	        // ── 3. Parse payload (JSON, form-encoded, raw text, or URL query) ──
    67	        // Delevo Express sends GET with templated URL params — merge query string
    68	        // into the payload so a single extractor handles all providers.
    69	        const contentType = req.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    70	        let payload: any = {};
    71	        // First, harvest query params (works for any method).
    72	        for (const [k, v] of url.searchParams.entries()) {
    73	            try { payload[k] = JSON.parse(v); } catch { payload[k] = v; }
    74	        }
    75	        // Then layer the body on top (if any).
    76	        if (req.method !== 'GET') {
    77	            if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
    78	                try { Object.assign(payload, await req.json()); } catch { /* empty body */ }
    79	            } else if (contentType.includes('form') || contentType.includes('multipart')) {
    80	                const formData = await req.formData();
    81	                for (const [key, value] of formData.entries()) {
    82	                    try { payload[key] = JSON.parse(value as string); } catch { payload[key] = value; }
    83	                }
    84	            } else {
    85	                const text = await req.text();
    86	                if (text) {
    87	                    try { Object.assign(payload, JSON.parse(text)); } catch { payload.raw = text; }
    88	                }
    89	            }
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        console.log(`[webhook] provider=${providerId} payload=`, JSON.stringify(payload));
    93	
    94	        // ── 4. Validate webhook credentials ─────────────────────────────
    95	        const { data: webhookConfig, error: webhookError } = await supabase
    96	            .from('provider_webhooks')
    97	            .select('*, "DigiCom Business", providers(provider_type)')
    98	            .eq('webhook_secret', webhookSecret)
    99	            .eq('provider_id', providerId)
   100	            .eq('is_active', true)
   101	            .single();
   102	
   103	        if (webhookError || !webhookConfig) {
   104	            console.error('[webhook] auth failed:', webhookError?.message);
   105	            return json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, 401);
   106	        }
   107	
   108	        const businessId = webhookConfig['DigiCom Business'];
   109	        // provider_type lives on providers, not provider_webhooks — joined above for the
   110	        // unmatched-status queue (log_unmatched_provider_status).
   111	        const providerType: string | null = webhookConfig.providers?.provider_type ?? null;
   112	
   113	        // ── 5. Extract tracking code (Coliaty=TRACKING, AMEEX=CODE) ─────
   114	        // Forcelog nests fields under `data` — extract defensively without disturbing flat providers.
   115	        const forcelogData = (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && payload.data && typeof payload.data === 'object')
   116	            ? payload.data
   117	            : null;
   118	        // Sendit payload shape is TBD — handle both wrapped (payload.data) and flat (payload.code).
   119	        // Sendit delivery codes start with "D" (e.g. "DH1D31F9029"), distinguishable from Forcelog's "F-XXX".
   120	        const trackingCode =
   121	            payload.CODE ||        // AMEEX
   122	            payload.TRACKING ||    // Coliaty
   123	            payload.tracking_number ||
   124	            payload.barcode ||
   125	            payload.ref ||
   126	            forcelogData?.parcel_code ||  // Forcelog
   127	            payload.code ||                // Sendit (flat)
   128	            forcelogData?.code ||          // Sendit (wrapped in data)
   129	            payload.tracking ||            // Digylog (flat camelCase)
   130	            payload.traking;               // Digylog typo per public docs
   131	
   132	        // Always log — even if no tracking code (so we can debug)
   133	        const { data: logEntry } = await supabase.from('webhook_logs').insert({
   134	            provider_id: providerId,
   135	            tracking_code: trackingCode || 'UNKNOWN',
   136	            payload,
   137	            processed: false,
   138	            'DigiCom Business': businessId,
   139	        }).select().single();
   140	
   141	        // Update last-received heartbeat
   142	        await supabase
   143	            .from('provider_webhooks')
   144	            .update({ last_received_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   145	            .eq('id', webhookConfig.id);
   146	
   147	        if (!trackingCode) {
   148	            console.warn('[webhook] no tracking code in payload');
   149	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: 'No tracking code in payload' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   150	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'No tracking code' });
   151	        }
   152	
   153	        console.log(`[webhook] tracking=${trackingCode} business=${businessId}`);
   154	
   155	        // ── 6. Find parcel → order ──────────────────────────────────────
   156	        // Primary: company_parcels.tracking_code
   157	        const { data: parcel } = await supabase
   158	            .from('company_parcels')
   159	            .select('id, order_id, provider_id')
   160	            .eq('tracking_code', trackingCode)
   161	            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   162	            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   163	            .limit(1)
   164	            .maybeSingle();
   165	
   166	        // Parcel is the sole source of truth for tracking→order resolution.
   167	        // (The legacy orders.tracking_number fallback was removed; that column is dropped.)
   168	        const orderId: string | null = parcel?.order_id || null;
   169	
   170	        if (!orderId) {
   171	            console.warn(`[webhook] order not found for tracking=${trackingCode}`);
   172	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: 'Order not found for tracking code' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   173	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'Order not found' });
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        // Skip soft-deleted orders entirely — don't leak status updates into trashed rows.
   177	        // The parcel lookup above hits company_parcels.tracking_code without join, so deleted
   178	        // orders can still surface here.
   179	        const { data: orderMeta, error: orderMetaErr } = await supabase
   180	            .from('orders')
   181	            .select('id, date_deleted, delivered, payed, date_livraison, package_status_id, replace')
   182	            .eq('id', orderId)
   183	            .maybeSingle();
   184	
   185	        if (orderMetaErr || !orderMeta) {
   186	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: `Order lookup failed: ${orderMetaErr?.message || 'not found'}` }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   187	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'Order lookup failed' });
   188	        }
   189	        if (orderMeta.date_deleted) {
   190	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Order is soft-deleted — skipped' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   190	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Order is soft-deleted — skipped' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   191	            return json({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'order_deleted' });
   192	        }
   193	
   194	        console.log(`[webhook] orderId=${orderId}`);
   195	
   196	        // ── 7. Coliaty event classification ─────────────────────────────
   197	        // Coliaty now distinguishes two events. PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED is the live lifecycle
   198	        // event (delivered, picked_up, …). PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED fires later when the
   199	        // invoice for that parcel is settled — Coliaty re-attaches the final STATUS
   200	        // (DELIVERED / REFUSE) along with billing fields (INVOICE_REF, PRICE, FEES, NET).
   201	        // The two MUST be handled differently: a SITUATION=PAID event arrives days after the
   202	        // actual delivery and must NOT overwrite date_livraison.
   203	        const eventName: string = String(payload.EVENT || '').toUpperCase();
   204	        const situation: string = String(payload.SITUATION || '').toUpperCase();
   205	        const isSituationEvent = eventName === 'PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED';
   206	        const isPaidSituation = situation === 'PAID';
   207	
   208	        // Pre-extract the webhook's own DATE so we can use it for date_livraison (delivery day)
   209	        // when Coliaty includes it on the lifecycle event itself.
   210	        const coliatyDate = parseColiatyDate(payload.DATE);
   211	
   212	        // ── 8. Multi-candidate status matching ──────────────────────────
   213	        // Priority: AMEEX sub-status > AMEEX/Coliaty main status > generic > Forcelog (data.secondary_status > data.status)
   214	        // Sendit uses camelCase keys — `newStatus` for forward flow, `statusReturn` for returns.
   215	        // `statusReturn === 'NA'` means no return event; skip it (filter below drops literal 'NA' too).
   216	        const skipSenditNA = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'NA' ? null : v);
   217	        // PAID is not a real Coliaty status code (it's a SITUATION). Old Coliaty payloads
   218	        // briefly mis-shipped EVENT=PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED, STATUS=PAID — those events should
   219	        // be treated as billing confirmations, not status changes. Drop "PAID" from the
   220	        // status-mapping candidates so we don't error or wrongly downgrade the order.
   221	        const dropPaid = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'PAID' ? null : v);
   222	
   223	        // ── Demand reconciliation (TKT-8b) ────────────────────────────────
   224	        // Coliaty documents NO webhook for parcel-change demands. Tolerantly
   225	        // treat any payload referencing a parcel_change_demands ref (PCD-...)
   226	        // — or an EVENT naming DEMAND/CHANGE_DEMAND even without a ref — as a
   227	        // reconciliation signal. Entirely fail-open: a bug here must never
   228	        // break the normal status pipeline below.
   229	        try {
   230	            const payloadStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
   231	            // No trailing hyphen in the capture; refs normalized uppercase so the
   232	            // case-sensitive .eq('ref', …) below always matches the stored form.
   233	            const demandRefs = Array.from(new Set(
   234	                (payloadStr.match(/PCD-\d+(?:-\d+)*/gi) || []).map((r) => r.toUpperCase())
   235	            ));
   236	            const isDemandSignal = demandRefs.length > 0 || /DEMAND|CHANGE_DEMAND/.test(eventName);
   237	
   238	            if (isDemandSignal) {
   239	                // Verdict from STATUS-ish fields first; whole-payload scan only as a
   240	                // fallback, so free-text like "client accepted delivery" in a comment
   241	                // can't force APPROVED when a real status field is present.
   242	                const verdictSource = String(
   243	                    payload.DEMAND_STATUS ?? payload.STATUS ?? payload.STATUT ??
   244	                    payload.SITUATION ?? forcelogData?.status ?? ''
   245	                ).toLowerCase();
   246	                const scan = verdictSource || payloadStr.toLowerCase();
   247	                const verdict = /approuv|approved|accept/.test(scan)
   248	                    ? 'APPROVED'
   249	                    : /refus|reject|denied/.test(scan)
   250	                        ? 'REJECTED'
   251	                        : null;
   252	                const providerStatusRaw =
   253	                    payload.STATUS || payload.STATUT || payload.SITUATION ||
   254	                    forcelogData?.status || eventName || null;
   255	                const baseUpdate: any = {
   256	                    provider_status_raw: providerStatusRaw,
   257	                    resolved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   258	                    webhook_log_id: logEntry?.id ?? null,
   259	                };
   260	
   261	                for (const ref of demandRefs) {
   262	                    if (verdict) {
   263	                        // First conclusion wins: a replayed/late webhook must never flip
   264	                        // an already-concluded verdict (REJECTED→APPROVED or vice versa).
   265	                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
   266	                            .update({ ...baseUpdate, status: verdict })
   267	                            .eq('ref', ref)
   268	                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   269	                            .in('status', ['PENDING', 'UNKNOWN']);
   270	                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
   271	                            .update(baseUpdate)
   272	                            .eq('ref', ref)
   273	                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   274	                            .in('status', ['APPROVED', 'REJECTED']);
   275	                    } else {
   276	                        // No recognizable verdict: only mark UNKNOWN if still PENDING —
   277	                        // never downgrade a demand already concluded APPROVED/REJECTED.
   278	                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
   279	                            .update({ ...baseUpdate, status: 'UNKNOWN' })
   280	                            .eq('ref', ref)
   281	                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   282	                            .eq('status', 'PENDING');
   283	                        await supabase.from('parcel_change_demands')
   284	                            .update(baseUpdate)
   285	                            .eq('ref', ref)
   286	                            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   287	                            .neq('status', 'PENDING');
   288	                    }
   289	                }
   290	
   291	                // Reuse the same status-candidate extraction the normal pipeline
   292	                // builds below (~statusCandidates) to decide whether this payload
   293	                // ALSO carries a real status update alongside the demand signal.
   294	                const demandStatusCandidates = [
   295	                    payload.STATUT_S,
   296	                    payload.STATUT,
   297	                    dropPaid(payload.STATUS),
   298	                    skipSenditNA(payload.statusReturn),
   299	                    payload.newStatus,
   300	                    payload.status,
   301	                    payload.status_return,
   302	                    forcelogData?.secondary_status,
   303	                    forcelogData?.status,
   304	                    forcelogData?.status_return,
   305	                    skipSenditNA(forcelogData?.statusReturn),
   306	                    forcelogData?.newStatus,
   307	                ].filter(Boolean);
   308	
   309	                if (demandStatusCandidates.length === 0) {
   310	                    if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Demand reconciliation only' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   311	                    return json({ status: 'demand_reconciled', refs: demandRefs });
   312	                }
   313	            }
   314	        } catch (demandErr) {
   315	            console.error('[webhook] demand reconciliation failed:', demandErr);
   316	        }
   317	
   318	        const statusCandidates = [
   319	            payload.STATUT_S,                         // AMEEX sub-status (most precise)
   320	            payload.STATUT,                           // AMEEX main status
   321	            dropPaid(payload.STATUS),                 // Coliaty status (drop literal "PAID")
   322	            skipSenditNA(payload.statusReturn),       // Sendit return-flow (camelCase, flat)
   323	            payload.newStatus,                        // Sendit forward-flow (camelCase, flat)
   324	            payload.status,                           // generic / legacy snake_case
   325	            payload.status_return,                    // legacy snake_case return
   326	            forcelogData?.secondary_status,           // Forcelog sub-status (more precise)
   327	            forcelogData?.status,                     // Forcelog primary code
   328	            forcelogData?.status_return,              // legacy wrapped return
   329	            skipSenditNA(forcelogData?.statusReturn), // Sendit wrapped return (defensive)
   330	            forcelogData?.newStatus,                  // Sendit wrapped forward (defensive)
   331	        ].filter(Boolean);
   332	
   333	        // PAID-only event (no real status field) — record the payment, skip status mapping.
   334	        if (statusCandidates.length === 0 && isPaidSituation) {
   335	            const billingUpdate: any = {
   336	                payed: true,
   337	                updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   338	            };
   339	            await supabase
   340	                .from('orders')
   341	                .update(billingUpdate)
   342	                .eq('id', orderId)
   343	                .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   344	                .is('date_deleted', null);
   345	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   346	            console.log(`[webhook] PAID-only situation recorded for order=${orderId}`);
   347	            return json({ status: 'success', situation: 'PAID', order_id: orderId });
   348	        }
   349	
   350	        if (statusCandidates.length === 0) {
   351	            // Legacy stale Coliaty payload where STATUS=PAID was the only signal — degrade
   352	            // gracefully: mark order as paid if SITUATION=PAID can be inferred from COMMENT.
   353	            const commentHint = String(payload.COMMENT || '').toLowerCase();
   354	            if (commentHint.includes('facture') && commentHint.includes('pay')) {
   355	                await supabase
   356	                    .from('orders')
   357	                    .update({ payed: true, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   358	                    .eq('id', orderId)
   359	                    .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   360	                    .is('date_deleted', null);
   361	                if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Inferred PAID from comment' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   362	                return json({ status: 'success', inferred: 'PAID', order_id: orderId });
   363	            }
   364	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: 'No status field in payload' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   365	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'Status missing' });
   366	        }
   367	
   368	        console.log(`[webhook] statusCandidates=`, statusCandidates);
   369	
   370	        let matchedStatus: any = null;
   371	        let matchedCandidate: string | null = null;
   372	
   373	        // AMEEX uses IN_PROGRESS as a container ("check the sub-status"); Forcelog uses it as a real in-transit state.
   374	        const isAmeexPayload = Boolean(payload.STATUT_S || payload.STATUT);
   375	
   376	        for (const candidate of statusCandidates) {
   377	            // Skip bare IN_PROGRESS — AMEEX container, not a real status. Forcelog sends IN_PROGRESS as a real code.
   378	            if (candidate === 'IN_PROGRESS' && isAmeexPayload) continue;
   379	
   380	            // Try RPC first (handles diacritics, case-insensitive, deterministic ordering)
   381	            const { data: rpcMatch, error: rpcErr } = await supabase
   382	                .rpc('match_package_status', { p_status_value: candidate });
   383	
   384	            if (!rpcErr && rpcMatch?.[0]) {
   385	                matchedStatus = rpcMatch[0];
   386	                matchedCandidate = candidate;
   387	                break;
   388	            }
   389	
   390	            // Fallback: JSONB array containment
   390	            // Fallback: JSONB array containment
   391	            const { data: fbMatch } = await supabase
   392	                .from('package_statuses')
   393	                .select('id, code, label, color')
   394	                .contains('delivery_service_statuses', JSON.stringify([candidate]))
   395	                .limit(1)
   396	                .maybeSingle();
   397	
   398	            if (fbMatch) {
   399	                matchedStatus = fbMatch;
   400	                matchedCandidate = candidate;
   401	                break;
   402	            }
   403	        }
   404	
   405	        if (!matchedStatus) {
   406	            const tried = statusCandidates.filter(c => c !== 'IN_PROGRESS').join(', ');
   407	            console.warn(`[webhook] no mapping for candidates: ${tried}`);
   408	            // No master match → status_id is left untouched (the parcel keeps its current
   409	            // status_id). Capture the VERBATIM primary raw status instead — the first
   410	            // candidate that isn't the AMEEX synthetic IN_PROGRESS container (same skip rule
   411	            // as the matching loop above) — on the parcel and in the unmatched-status queue,
   412	            // for owner triage/mapping. Both writes are fail-open: a failure here must not
   413	            // block the rest of this handler's existing flow.
   414	            const rawStatusValue = String(
   415	                statusCandidates.find((c) => !(c === 'IN_PROGRESS' && isAmeexPayload)) ?? statusCandidates[0]
   416	            );
   417	            if (parcel) {
   418	                try {
   419	                    const { error: rawStatusErr } = await supabase
   420	                        .from('company_parcels')
   421	                        .update({ raw_provider_status: rawStatusValue })
   422	                        .eq('id', parcel.id);
   423	                    if (rawStatusErr) console.warn('[webhook] failed to store raw_provider_status:', rawStatusErr.message);
   424	                } catch (e) {
   425	                    console.warn('[webhook] raw_provider_status update threw:', e);
   426	                }
   427	            }
   428	            try {
   429	                const { error: queueErr } = await supabase.rpc('log_unmatched_provider_status', {
   430	                    p_provider_type: providerType,
   431	                    p_raw_status: rawStatusValue,
   432	                });
   433	                if (queueErr) console.warn('[webhook] log_unmatched_provider_status failed:', queueErr.message);
   434	            } catch (e) {
   435	                console.warn('[webhook] log_unmatched_provider_status threw:', e);
   436	            }
   437	            // If this was a paid-situation event with an unknown status, at least flag payed=true.
   438	            if (isPaidSituation) {
   439	                await supabase
   440	                    .from('orders')
   441	                    .update({ payed: true, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   442	                    .eq('id', orderId)
   443	                    .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   444	                    .is('date_deleted', null);
   445	            }
   446	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: `No mapping for: ${tried}` }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   447	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'No mapping found' });
   448	        }
   449	
   450	        console.log(`[webhook] matched: "${matchedCandidate}" → ${matchedStatus.label} (${matchedStatus.code})`);
   451	
   452	        // ── 9. Regression guard (semantic flags, not category strings) ──
   453	        // Fetch the CURRENT status's flags. Deleted-order short-circuit above guarantees the row exists.
   454	        const FLAG_COLS = 'is_delivered, is_returned, is_cancelled, is_postponed, is_new, is_terminal, flow_rank';
   455	        let current: any = null;
   456	        if (orderMeta.package_status_id) {
   457	            const { data: curFlags } = await supabase
   458	                .from('package_statuses')
   459	                .select(FLAG_COLS)
   460	                .eq('id', orderMeta.package_status_id)
   461	                .maybeSingle();
   462	            current = curFlags || null;
   463	        }
   464	
   465	        // The matched (new) status's flags. match_package_status / the fallback return only
   466	        // id/code/label/color, so read the semantic flags by id.
   467	        const { data: newFlags } = await supabase
   468	            .from('package_statuses')
   469	            .select(FLAG_COLS)
   470	            .eq('id', matchedStatus.id)
   471	            .maybeSingle();
   472	        const nf: any = newFlags || {};
   473	
   474	        if (current) {
   475	            // Never regress to a 'new' status from any non-new status.
   476	            if (nf.is_new && !current.is_new) {
   477	                console.log(`[webhook] BLOCKED regression to 'new'`);
   478	                if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Regression blocked → new' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   479	                return json({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'regression_blocked' });
   480	            }
   481	
   482	            // Terminal lock: once delivered/returned/refused/cancelled, only allow same-class
   483	            // updates (idempotent). Class = the (delivered, returned, cancelled) flag triple;
   484	            // returned/returning/refused share is_returned, so they count as one return-class.
   485	            // Exchange orders (replace=true) get the single delivered → returned transition.
   486	            const sameClass =
   487	                current.is_delivered === nf.is_delivered &&
   488	                current.is_returned === nf.is_returned &&
   489	                current.is_cancelled === nf.is_cancelled;
   490	            const exchangeDeliveredToReturned =
   491	                current.is_delivered && nf.is_returned && orderMeta.replace === true;
   492	            if (current.is_terminal && !sameClass && !exchangeDeliveredToReturned) {
   493	                console.log(`[webhook] BLOCKED terminal regression`);
   494	                if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Terminal lock' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   495	                if (isPaidSituation) {
   496	                    await supabase
   497	                        .from('orders')
   498	                        .update({ payed: true, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   499	                        .eq('id', orderId)
   500	                        .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   501	                        .is('date_deleted', null);
   502	                }
   503	                return json({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'terminal_lock' });
   504	            }
   505	
   506	            // Never move backward in the forward delivery progression (flow_rank ordinal).
   507	            if (current.flow_rank != null && nf.flow_rank != null && nf.flow_rank < current.flow_rank) {
   508	                console.log(`[webhook] BLOCKED backward move: ${current.flow_rank} → ${nf.flow_rank}`);
   509	                if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: `Backward blocked: ${current.flow_rank} → ${nf.flow_rank}` }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   510	                return json({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'backward_blocked' });
   511	            }
   512	        }
   513	
   514	        // ── 10. Same-status no-op guard ─────────────────────────────────
   515	        // Coliaty (and other providers) occasionally retry the same webhook event, and
   516	        // each retry without this check would bump updated_at — triggering a realtime
   517	        // UPDATE, cache invalidation, and a redundant toast even though nothing actually
   518	        // changed. Bail out unless:
   519	        //   - the status_id actually moved, OR
   520	        //   - we'd be flipping the delivered flag on for the first time, OR
   521	        //   - we'd be filing the first cancellation / postponement timestamp, OR
   522	        //   - SITUATION=PAID newly marks the order as payed.
   523	        const sameStatusId = orderMeta.package_status_id === matchedStatus.id;
   524	        const hasDeliveredSideEffect = nf.is_delivered && !orderMeta.delivered;
   525	        const hasCancelSideEffect = nf.is_cancelled && !(current && current.is_cancelled);
   526	        const hasPostponeSideEffect = nf.is_postponed && !(current && current.is_postponed);
   527	        const hasPaidSideEffect = isPaidSituation && !orderMeta.payed;
   528	        const noOp =
   529	            sameStatusId &&
   530	            !hasDeliveredSideEffect &&
   531	            !hasCancelSideEffect &&
   532	            !hasPostponeSideEffect &&
   533	            !hasPaidSideEffect;
   534	
   535	        if (noOp) {
   536	            // status_id already equals matchedStatus.id here (sameStatusId) and label/color
   537	            // are derived from the master, so there is nothing to reconcile on the parcel.
   538	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'No-op (status unchanged)' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   539	            console.log(`[webhook] NO-OP order=${orderId} → ${matchedStatus.code}`);
   540	            return json({ status: 'noop', reason: 'status_unchanged', order_id: orderId });
   541	        }
   542	
   543	        // ── 11. Update company_parcels ──────────────────────────────────
   544	        if (parcel) {
   545	            await supabase.from('company_parcels').update({
   546	                status_id: matchedStatus.id,
   547	                raw_provider_status: null,
   548	                updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   549	            }).eq('id', parcel.id);
   550	        }
   551	
   552	        // ── 12. Update order ────────────────────────────────────────────
   553	        const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
   554	        const updateData: any = {
   555	            package_status_id: matchedStatus.id,
   556	            updated_at: nowIso,
   557	        };
   558	
   559	        // Delivered: mark delivered=true and lock the delivery date. The date sources, in
   560	        // priority order:
   561	        //   1. Existing orders.date_livraison if it's already set (idempotent — don't move it)
   562	        //   2. Coliaty payload DATE field (parsed dd/mm/yyyy or dd-mm-yyyy at 12:00 UTC)
   563	        //   3. nowIso (live PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED with no DATE — webhook arrival is the best
   564	        //      estimator for the actual delivery moment)
   565	        // For PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED, the DATE is the invoice-pay day and is NOT used for
   566	        // date_livraison — the terminal-category lock above already short-circuits this code
   567	        // path for already-delivered orders. For brand-new deliveries first observed via the
   568	        // situation event (no prior PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED), we fall back to nowIso.
   569	        if (nf.is_delivered) {
   570	            updateData.delivered = true;
   571	            if (orderMeta.date_livraison) {
   572	                // Already set — don't move it.
   573	            } else if (!isSituationEvent && coliatyDate) {
   574	                updateData.date_livraison = coliatyDate;
   575	            } else {
   576	                updateData.date_livraison = nowIso;
   577	            }
   578	        }
   579	
   580	        // Cancelled / postponed timestamps — set on first transition, idempotent.
   581	        if (nf.is_cancelled && !(current && current.is_cancelled)) {
   582	            updateData.annulation_date = coliatyDate || nowIso;
   583	        }
   584	        if (nf.is_postponed && !(current && current.is_postponed)) {
   585	            updateData.reported_date = coliatyDate || nowIso;
   586	        }
   587	
   588	        // Paid situation — always flag the order, regardless of status transition outcome.
   589	        if (isPaidSituation) {
   590	            updateData.payed = true;
   591	        }
   592	
   593	        const { error: updateError } = await supabase
   594	            .from('orders')
   595	            .update(updateData)
   596	            .eq('id', orderId)
   597	            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   598	            .is('date_deleted', null);
   599	
   600	        if (updateError) {
   601	            console.error('[webhook] order update failed:', updateError.message);
   602	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: updateError.message }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   603	            return json({ error: 'Update failed' }, 500);
   604	        }
   605	
   606	        // ── 12. Mark success ────────────────────────────────────────────
   607	        if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   608	
   609	        console.log(`[webhook] SUCCESS order=${orderId} → ${matchedStatus.label} (${matchedStatus.code})${isSituationEvent ? ' [situation]' : ''}${isPaidSituation ? ' [paid]' : ''}`);
   610	        return json({
   611	            status: 'success',
   612	            status_mapped: matchedStatus.code,
   613	            order_id: orderId,
   614	            event: eventName || null,
   615	            situation: isPaidSituation ? 'PAID' : null,
   616	        });
   617	
   618	    } catch (e) {
   619	        console.error('[webhook] fatal:', e);
   620	        return json({ error: e.message }, 500);
   621	    }
   622	});

   195	
   196	export class ColiatyAdapter {
   197	    // Fully migrated to the customer/public API (https://customer-api-v1.coliaty.com,
   198	    // Bearer {public}:{secret}, NO /api/client prefix). Pickup-note create/add/remove/
   199	    // labels are now public — the old dashboard (app.coliaty.com, email/password JWT) is
   200	    // no longer used at all, removing the api_login/api_password dependency and the
   201	    // Cloudflare-challenge exposure on app.coliaty.com.
   202	    constructor(credentials) {
   203	        this.baseUrl = credentials.api_base_url || 'https://customer-api-v1.coliaty.com';
   204	        this.apiKeyPublic = credentials.api_public_key || '';
   205	        this.apiKeySecret = credentials.api_secret_key || '';
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    getAuthHeader() {
   209	        return `Bearer ${this.apiKeyPublic}:${this.apiKeySecret}`;
   210	    }
   211	
   212	    // Coliaty requires package_content between 5 and 100 characters.
   213	    // The wizard sends "CODE xN, CODE xN" which can blow past 100 chars on
   214	    // multi-SKU orders. Fall back to "{N} produits, {M} pieces" derived from
   215	    // the same string so we don't lose item/piece counts.
   216	    buildPackageContent(productInfo) {
   217	        const MIN = 5;
   218	        const MAX = 100;
   219	        const raw = String(productInfo || '').trim();
   220	
   221	        if (raw.length >= MIN && raw.length <= MAX) return raw;
   222	
   223	        let itemCount = 0;
   224	        let totalPieces = 0;
   225	        if (raw) {
   226	            const parts = raw.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
   227	            itemCount = parts.length;
   228	            for (const p of parts) {
   229	                const m = p.match(/x(\d+)\s*$/i);
   230	                if (m) totalPieces += parseInt(m[1], 10);
   231	            }
   232	        }
   233	
   234	        if (itemCount > 0) {
   235	            const productLabel = itemCount === 1 ? 'produit' : 'produits';
   236	            const pieceLabel = totalPieces === 1 ? 'piece' : 'pieces';
   237	            const summary = totalPieces > 0
   238	                ? `${itemCount} ${productLabel}, ${totalPieces} ${pieceLabel}`
   239	                : `${itemCount} ${productLabel}`;
   240	            if (summary.length >= MIN && summary.length <= MAX) return summary;
   241	            if (summary.length > MAX) return summary.substring(0, MAX);
   242	        }
   243	
   244	        if (raw.length > MAX) return raw.substring(0, MAX);
   245	        return 'Colis';
   246	    }
   247	
   248	    async trackParcel(code, externalId) {
   249	        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/parcel/history/${code}`, {
   250	            headers: { Authorization: this.getAuthHeader() }
   251	        });
   252	        if (!response.ok) {
   253	            const errorText = await response.text();
   254	            throw new Error(`Failed to track parcel: ${errorText}`);
   255	        }
   256	        const result = await response.json();
   257	        // Coliaty returns { success, data: { package_code, details: [] } }
   258	        const details = result.data?.details || [];
   259	
   260	        // Transform Coliaty format to match frontend expectations.
   261	        // Phone candidates cover Coliaty payload variations across livreur/actor
   262	        // shapes (phone, mobile, tel, gsm). The frontend renders it as a tel: link.
   263	        const pickPhone = (src: any): string => {
   264	            if (!src) return '';
   265	            return String(src.phone || src.mobile || src.tel || src.gsm || '').trim();
   266	        };
   267	        const history = details.map((event: any) => {
   268	            const livreur = event.HISTORY_LIVREUR || null;
   269	            const actor = event.HISTORY_ACTOR || null;
   270	            return {
   271	                status: event.HISTORY_STATUS,
   272	                date: event.HISTORY_STATUS_DATE
   273	                    ? new Date(parseInt(event.HISTORY_STATUS_DATE) * 1000).toISOString()
   274	                    : (event.HISTORY_TIMESTAMP
   275	                        ? new Date(parseInt(event.HISTORY_TIMESTAMP) * 1000).toISOString()
   276	                        : null),
   277	                comment: event.HISTORY_COMMENT || '',
   278	                user: livreur?.name || actor?.name || '',
   279	                phone: pickPhone(livreur) || pickPhone(actor),
   280	                _original: event
   281	            };
   282	        });
   283	
   284	        // Get current status from the latest event
   285	        const latestEvent = history[0] || {};
   286	
   287	        return {
   288	            status: latestEvent.status || 'Unknown',
   289	            statusLabel: latestEvent.status || 'Unknown',
   290	            history: history,
   291	            _original: result
   292	        };
   293	    }
   294

   296	    // Resolves the destination city via provider_cities the same way both paths need.

   300	            package_reciever: parcelData.receiver_name || parcelData.customer_name,

   142	        await supabase

   144	            .update({ last_received_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   145	            .eq('id', webhookConfig.id);
   146

   149	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: 'No tracking code in payload' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   150	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'No tracking code' });
   151	        }
   152	
   153	        console.log(`[webhook] tracking=${trackingCode} business=${businessId}`);
   154	
   155	        // ── 6. Find parcel → order ──────────────────────────────────────
   156	        // Primary: company_parcels.tracking_code
   157	        const { data: parcel } = await supabase
   158	            .from('company_parcels')
   159	            .select('id, order_id, provider_id')
   160	            .eq('tracking_code', trackingCode)
   161	            .eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)
   162	            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   163	            .limit(1)
   164	            .maybeSingle();
   165	
   166	        // Parcel is the sole source of truth for tracking→order resolution.
   167	        // (The legacy orders.tracking_number fallback was removed; that column is dropped.)
   168	        const orderId: string | null = parcel?.order_id || null;
   169	
   170	        if (!orderId) {
   171	            console.warn(`[webhook] order not found for tracking=${trackingCode}`);
   172	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: 'Order not found for tracking code' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   173	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'Order not found' });
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        // Skip soft-deleted orders entirely — don't leak status updates into trashed rows.
   177	        // The parcel lookup above hits company_parcels.tracking_code without join, so deleted
   178	        // orders can still surface here.
   179	        const { data: orderMeta, error: orderMetaErr } = await supabase
   180	            .from('orders')
   181	            .select('id, date_deleted, delivered, payed, date_livraison, package_status_id, replace')
   182	            .eq('id', orderId)
   183	            .maybeSingle();
   184	
   185	        if (orderMetaErr || !orderMeta) {
   186	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ error_message: `Order lookup failed: ${orderMetaErr?.message || 'not found'}` }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   187	            return json({ status: 'logged', message: 'Order lookup failed' });
   188	        }
   189	        if (orderMeta.date_deleted) {
   190	            if (logEntry) await supabase.from('webhook_logs').update({ processed: true, error_message: 'Order is soft-deleted — skipped' }).eq('id', logEntry.id);
   191	            return json({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'order_deleted' });
   192	        }
   193	
   194	        console.log(`[webhook] orderId=${orderId}`);
   195	
   196	        // ── 7. Coliaty event classification ─────────────────────────────
   197	        // Coliaty now distinguishes two events. PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED is the live lifecycle
   198	        // event (delivered, picked_up, …). PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED fires later when the
   199	        // invoice for that parcel is settled — Coliaty re-attaches the final STATUS
   200	        // (DELIVERED / REFUSE) along with billing fields (INVOICE_REF, PRICE, FEES, NET).
   201	        // The two MUST be handled differently: a SITUATION=PAID event arrives days after the
   202	        // actual delivery and must NOT overwrite date_livraison.
   203	        const eventName: string = String(payload.EVENT || '').toUpperCase();
   204	        const situation: string = String(payload.SITUATION || '').toUpperCase();
   205	        const isSituationEvent = eventName === 'PARCEL_SITUATION_CHANGED';
   206	        const isPaidSituation = situation === 'PAID';
   207	
   208	        // Pre-extract the webhook's own DATE so we can use it for date_livraison (delivery day)
   209	        // when Coliaty includes it on the lifecycle event itself.
   210	        const coliatyDate = parseColiatyDate(payload.DATE);
   211	
   212	        // ── 8. Multi-candidate status matching ──────────────────────────
   213	        // Priority: AMEEX sub-status > AMEEX/Coliaty main status > generic > Forcelog (data.secondary_status > data.status)
   214	        // Sendit uses camelCase keys — `newStatus` for forward flow, `statusReturn` for returns.
   215	        // `statusReturn === 'NA'` means no return event; skip it (filter below drops literal 'NA' too).
   216	        const skipSenditNA = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'NA' ? null : v);
   217	        // PAID is not a real Coliaty status code (it's a SITUATION). Old Coliaty payloads
   218	        // briefly mis-shipped EVENT=PARCEL_STATUS_CHANGED, STATUS=PAID — those events should
   219	        // be treated as billing confirmations, not status changes. Drop "PAID" from the
   220	        // status-mapping candidates so we don't error or wrongly downgrade the order.
   221	        const dropPaid = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.toUpperCase() === 'PAID' ? null : v);
   222	
   223	        // ── Demand reconciliation (TKT-8b) ────────────────────────────────
   224	        // Coliaty documents NO webhook for parcel-change demands. Tolerantly
   225	        // treat any payload referencing a parcel_change_demands ref (PCD-...)
   226	        // — or an EVENT naming DEMAND/CHANGE_DEMAND even without a ref — as a
   227	        // reconciliation signal. Entirely fail-open: a bug here must never
   228	        // break the normal status pipeline below.
   229	        try {
   230	            const payloadStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
    80	};
    81	
    82	/** Only the fields that actually changed — skip equal, both-empty, or absent-in-new. */
    83	const demandDiffs = (d: ChangeDemand) => {
    84	    const oldV = (d.old_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    85	    const newV = (d.new_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    86	    return DEMAND_FIELDS.flatMap(({ key, label }) => {
    87	        if (newV[key] === undefined) return [];
    88	        const next = demandText(newV[key]);
    89	        const prev = demandText(oldV[key]);
    90	        if (!next && !prev) return [];
    91	        // Numeric equality for the price only — a phone like 0612… must never be
    92	        // compared as a number (leading zero would make it equal to 612…).
    93	        const numeric = key === 'price' && prev !== '' && next !== ''
    94	            && Number.isFinite(Number(prev)) && Number.isFinite(Number(next));
    95	        if (numeric ? Number(prev) === Number(next) : prev === next) return [];
    96	        return [{ key, label, prev, next }];
    97	    });
    98	};
    99	
   100	const demandAuthor = (d: ChangeDemand): string => {
   101	    const u = d.created_by_user;
   102	    const row = Array.isArray(u) ? u[0] : u;
   103	    return (row?.name || '').trim();
   104	};
   105	
   106	export function ParcelTrackingDialog({ open, onOpenChange, orderId }: ParcelTrackingDialogProps) {
   107	    const { toast } = useToast();
   108	    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   109	    const [trackingCode, setTrackingCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
   110	    const [trackingHistory, setTrackingHistory] = useState<unknown>(null);
   111	    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
   112	
   113	    // Load all package_statuses for icon/color matching
   114	    const { data: pkgStatuses } = useQuery({
   115	        queryKey: ['package-statuses-icons'],
   116	        queryFn: async () => {
   117	            const { data } = await supabase
   118	                .from('package_statuses')
   119	                .select('id, code, label, color, icon, delivery_service_statuses')
   120	                .eq('active', true)
   121	                .eq('show_in_courier', true);
   122	            return (data || []) as unknown as PkgStatus[];
   123	        },
   124	        // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — without this gate the query fired on every
   125	        // page load of every host page with the dialog closed.
   126	        enabled: open,
   127	        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   128	    });
   129	
   130	    // Parcel-change demands we filed for this parcel. Additive + silent: on error
   131	    // the timeline renders exactly as it does today.
   132	    const { data: demands } = useQuery({
   133	        queryKey: ['parcel-change-demands', trackingCode],
   134	        queryFn: async () => {
   135	            const { data } = await (supabase as any)
   136	                .from('parcel_change_demands')
   137	                .select('id, ref, request_type, status, old_values, new_values, created_at, resolved_at, created_by_user:users!parcel_change_demands_created_by_fkey(name)')
   138	                .eq('tracking_code', trackingCode as string)
   139	                .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
   140	            return (data || []) as ChangeDemand[];
   141	        },
   142	        // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — gate on `open` (see the comment above).
   143	        enabled: open && !!trackingCode,
   144	        staleTime: 60 * 1000,
   145	    });
   146	
   147	    const hasApprovedDemand = (demands ?? []).some(d => d.status === 'APPROVED');
   148	
   149	    useEffect(() => {
   150	        if (!open || !orderId) {
   151	            setTrackingCode(null);
   152	            setTrackingHistory(null);
   153	            setIsLoading(false);
   154	            return;
   155	        }
   156	
   157	        let cancelled = false;
   158	
   159	        const run = async () => {
   160	            setIsLoading(true);
   161	            setTrackingHistory(null);
   162	            setTrackingCode(null);
   163	
   164	            try {
   165	                const { data: parcel, error } = await supabase
   166	                    .from('company_parcels')
   167	                    .select('id, tracking_code, external_id, provider_id, provider:providers(id, name, provider_type, logo_url)')
   168	                    .eq('order_id', orderId)
   169	                    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   170	                    .limit(1)
   171	                    .maybeSingle();
   172	
   173	                if (cancelled) return;
   174	                if (error) throw error;
   175	
   176	                if (!parcel?.tracking_code || !String(parcel.tracking_code).trim() || !parcel.provider_id) {
   177	                    const { data: courierParcel, error: courierErr } = await (supabase as any)
   178	                        .from('courier_parcels')
   179	                        .select('id, tracking_code, status_id, created_at, courier_id, status:package_statuses(label, color), courier:users!courier_parcels_courier_id_fkey(id, name, phone)')
   180	                        .eq('order_id', orderId)
   181	                        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   182	                        .limit(1)
   183	                        .maybeSingle();
   184	                    if (cancelled) return;
   185	                    if (courierErr) throw courierErr;
   186	
   187	                    if (!courierParcel?.tracking_code || !String(courierParcel.tracking_code).trim()) {
   188	                        toast({ title: 'Pas de suivi', description: 'Aucun colis avec code de suivi pour cette commande.', variant: 'destructive' });
   189	                        onOpenChange(false);
   190	                        return;
   191	                    }
   192	
   193	                    setTrackingCode(String(courierParcel.tracking_code).trim());
   194	                    const courierName = courierParcel.courier?.name || 'Coursier';
   195	                    const courierPhone = courierParcel.courier?.phone || '';
   196	                    const cpLabel = courierParcel.status?.label || 'Nouveau';
   197	                    const cpColor = courierParcel.status?.color || '#3b82f6';
   198	                    setTrackingHistory({
   199	                        statusLabel: cpLabel,
   200	                        statusColor: cpColor,
   201	                        history: [{
   202	                            status: cpLabel,
   203	                            statusLabel: cpLabel,
   204	                            date: courierParcel.created_at || null,
   205	                            comment: 'Colis créé en interne et confié à ' + courierName,
   205	                            comment: 'Colis créé en interne et confié à ' + courierName,
   206	                            color: cpColor,
   207	                            user: courierName,
   208	                            phone: courierPhone,
   209	                        }],
   210	                    });
   211	                    // D1 keys — the pre-D1 ['orders'] key matches nothing anymore (OR-9)
   212	                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-page'] });
   213	                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-kpis'] });
   214	                    return;
   215	                }
   216	
   217	                setTrackingCode(String(parcel.tracking_code).trim());
   218	
   219	                // Throttle (owner-approved 2026-07-17): a parcel refreshed in
   220	                // the last 10min replays its cached result — no external
   221	                // carrier call, no status re-write, no global invalidations.
   222	                const cached = getCachedTracking(String(parcel.tracking_code).trim());
   223	                if (cached) {
   224	                    setTrackingHistory(cached);
   225	                    return;
   226	                }
   227	
   228	                const data = await trackParcelWithFallback({
   229	                    id: parcel.id,
   230	                    provider_id: parcel.provider_id,
   231	                    tracking_code: String(parcel.tracking_code).trim(),
   232	                    external_id: parcel.external_id,
   233	                });
   234	
   235	                if (cancelled) return;
   236	                setTrackingHistory(data);
   237	
   238	                const providerStatus = (data.statusLabel || data.status || data.current_status) as string | undefined;
   239	
   240	                if (providerStatus) {
   241	                    const matched = await matchPackageStatus(supabase, providerStatus);
   242	                    // status_id is the only persisted parcel status; skip when unmatched
   243	                    // rather than wiping the existing status.
   244	                    if (matched?.id) {
   245	                        await supabase
   246	                            .from('company_parcels')
   247	                            .update({ status_id: matched.id })
   248	                            .eq('id', parcel.id);
   249	                    }
   250	                }
   251	                // D1 keys — the pre-D1 ['orders'] key matches nothing anymore (OR-9)
   252	                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-page'] });
   253	                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-kpis'] });
   254	            } catch (e: unknown) {
   255	                if (!cancelled) {
   256	                    toast({ title: 'Tracking Error', description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Échec du suivi', variant: 'destructive' });
   257	                    onOpenChange(false);
   258	                }
   259	            } finally {
   260	                if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false);
   261	            }
   262	        };
   263	
   264	        run();
   265	        return () => { cancelled = true; };
   266	    }, [open, orderId]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   267	
   268	    const statusMap: Record<string, string> = {
   269	        NEW_PARCEL: 'Nouveau', WAITING_PICKUP: 'En attente de ramassage', DISTRIBUTION: 'En cours de distribution',
   270	        DELIVERED: 'Livré', REFUSED: 'Refusé', REFUSE: 'Refusé', ANNULE: 'Annulé', CANCELLED: 'Annulé',
   271	        RETURNED: 'Retourné', RETOUR: 'Retourné', POSTPONED: 'Reporté', WAITING_PREPARATION: 'En préparation',
   272	        PICKED_UP: 'Ramassé', READY_FOR_PICKUP: 'Prêt pour ramassage', SENT: 'Expédié', RECEIVED: 'Reçu',
   273	        SCHEDULED: 'Programmé', PROGRAMMER: 'Programmé', NO_ANSWER: 'Pas de réponse', RELAUNCH: 'Relancer',
   274	    };
   275	
   276	    const situationMap: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
   277	        PAID: { label: 'Payé', className: 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green border-ant-green/30' },
   278	        NOT_PAID: { label: 'Non payé', className: 'bg-ant-orange/10 text-ant-orange border-ant-orange/30' },
   279	        INVOICED: { label: 'Facturé', className: 'bg-ant-blue/10 text-ant-blue border-blue-200' },
   280	    };
   281	
   282	    /** Match an event's raw status string to a package_statuses row for icon/color */
   283	    const matchPkgStatus = (rawStatus: string, statusLabel?: string): PkgStatus | null => {
   284	        if (!pkgStatuses?.length) return null;
   285	        const candidates = [statusLabel, rawStatus].filter(Boolean) as string[];
   286	        for (const candidate of candidates) {
   287	            const lower = candidate.toLowerCase();
   288	            // Match by delivery_service_statuses array
   289	            const byDss = pkgStatuses.find(ps =>
   290	                ps.delivery_service_statuses?.some(dss => dss.toLowerCase() === lower)
   291	            );
   292	            if (byDss) return byDss;
   293	            // Match by label
   294	            const byLabel = pkgStatuses.find(ps => ps.label.toLowerCase() === lower);
   295	            if (byLabel) return byLabel;
   296	            // Match by code
   297	            const byCode = pkgStatuses.find(ps => ps.code.toLowerCase() === lower);
   298	            if (byCode) return byCode;
   299	        }
   300	        return null;
   301	    };
   302	
   303	    return (
   304	        <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
   305	            <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[560px] rounded-[32px] border-none p-0 overflow-hidden bg-white">
   306	                <DialogHeader className="p-8 pb-0">
   307	                    <DialogTitle className="text-2xl font-black text-ant-ink flex items-center gap-3">
   308	                        <div className="p-2 bg-ant-ink/5 rounded-xl">
   309	                            <Truck className="w-5 h-5 text-ant-ink" />
   310	                        </div>
   311	                        Suivi du colis
   312	                    </DialogTitle>
   313	                    <DialogDescription className="font-bold text-ant-muted">
   314	                        Code: <span className="text-ant-ink">{trackingCode || '—'}</span>
   315	                        {hasApprovedDemand && (
   316	                            <span className="ml-2 align-middle inline-flex items-center text-[10px] font-semibold bg-ant-ink/5 text-ant-ink rounded-md px-2 py-0.5">
   317	                                Infos modifiées
   318	                            </span>
   319	                        )}
   320	                    </DialogDescription>
   321	                </DialogHeader>
   322	
   323	                <div className="p-8 max-h-[65vh] overflow-y-auto">
   324	                    {isLoading ? (
   325	                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 gap-4">
   326	                            <Loader2 className="w-10 h-10 text-ant-ink animate-spin" />
   327	                            <p className="font-bold text-ant-muted animate-pulse">Récupération des données...</p>
   328	                        </div>
   329	                    ) : trackingHistory ? (
   330	                        <div className="relative">
   331	                            {(() => {
   332	                                const th = trackingHistory as Record<string, unknown> | unknown[] | null;
   333	                                let events: unknown[] = Array.isArray(th)
   334	                                    ? th
   335	                                    : Array.isArray((th as Record<string, unknown>)?.history)
   336	                                      ? ((th as Record<string, unknown>).history as unknown[])
   337	                                      : [];
   338	                                if (events.length === 0 && th && !Array.isArray(th)) {
   339	                                    const o = th as Record<string, unknown>;
   340	                                    events = [{
   341	                                        status: o.statusLabel || o.status || 'Nouveau',
   342	                                        date: null,
   343	                                        comment: '',
   344	                                        statusLabel: o.statusLabel,
   345	                                        color: o.statusColor || o.color,
   346	                                    }];
   347	                                }
   348	
   349	                                // Reverse: oldest first (top) → newest last (bottom)
   350	                                const sorted = [...events].reverse();
   351	
   352	                                // Interleave the change demands by created_at. Events keep
   353	                                // their relative order; an event with no parseable date
   354	                                // inherits the last known timestamp so it stays anchored
   355	                                // next to its neighbours.
   356	                                type TimelineItem =
   357	                                    | { kind: 'event'; ts: number; ord: number; event: Record<string, unknown> }
   358	                                    | { kind: 'demand'; ts: number; ord: number; demand: ChangeDemand };
   359	                                let carried = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   360	                                const eventItems: TimelineItem[] = sorted.map((raw, i) => {
   361	                                    const ev = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
   362	                                    const d = ev.date ? new Date(ev.date as string) : null;
   363	                                    if (d && isValid(d)) carried = d.getTime();
   364	                                    return { kind: 'event', ts: carried, ord: i, event: ev };
   365	                                });
   366	                                const demandItems: TimelineItem[] = (demands ?? []).map((dm, i) => {
   367	                                    const d = new Date(dm.created_at);
   368	                                    return {
   369	                                        kind: 'demand',
   370	                                        ts: isValid(d) ? d.getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
   371	                                        ord: sorted.length + i,
   372	                                        demand: dm,
   373	                                    };
   374	                                });
   375	                                const merged = [...eventItems, ...demandItems].sort((a, b) =>
   376	                                    a.ts === b.ts ? a.ord - b.ord : a.ts < b.ts ? -1 : 1,
   377	                                );
   378	                                const lastEventOrd = sorted.length - 1;
   379	
   380	                                return (
   381	                                    <div className="relative pl-10">
   382	                                        {/* Vertical line */}
   383	                                        <div className="absolute left-[15px] top-4 bottom-4 w-0.5 bg-gradient-to-b from-ant-line via-ant-line to-ant-ink/30" />
   384	
   385	                                        <div className="space-y-0">
   386	                                            {merged.map((item: TimelineItem, idx: number) => {
   387	                                                if (item.kind === 'demand') {
   388	                                                    const dm = item.demand;
   389	                                                    const chip = DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP[dm.status] || DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP.UNKNOWN;
   390	                                                    const diffs = demandDiffs(dm);
   391	                                                    const author = demandAuthor(dm);
   392	                                                    const createdAt = new Date(dm.created_at);
   393	                                                    const resolvedAt = dm.resolved_at ? new Date(dm.resolved_at) : null;
   394	                                                    return (
   395	                                                        <div key={dm.id} className="relative pb-6 last:pb-0">
   396	                                                            {/* Timeline node */}
   397	                                                            <div className="absolute -left-10 top-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-[3px] border-white bg-ant-light text-ant-ink shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center z-10">
   398	                                                                {dm.request_type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' ? (
   399	                                                                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   400	                                                                ) : (
   401	                                                                    <PencilLine className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   402	                                                                )}
   403	                                                            </div>
   404	
   405	                                                            <div className="rounded-xl border border-ant-line bg-ant-paper px-3.5 py-3 space-y-2">
   406	                                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
   407	                                                                    <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-ant-ink">
   408	                                                                        {DEMAND_TYPE_LABEL[dm.request_type] || 'Demande de modification'}
   409	                                                                    </span>
   410	                                                                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${chip.className}`}>
   411	                                                                        {chip.label}
   412	                                                                    </span>
   413	                                                                    <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] tabular-nums text-ant-mid">{dm.ref}</span>
   414	                                                                </div>
   415	
   416	                                                                {diffs.length > 0 && (
   417	                                                                    <div className="space-y-1">
   418	                                                                        {diffs.map(diff => (
   419	                                                                            <div key={diff.key} className="flex items-start gap-2">
   420	                                                                                <span className="text-[10.5px] text-ant-mid w-24 shrink-0 pt-px">{diff.label}</span>
   421	                                                                                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-1.5">
   422	                                                                                    {diff.prev && (
   423	                                                                                        <>
   424	                                                                                            <span className="text-[11px] text-ant-mid line-through break-words">{diff.prev}</span>
   425	                                                                                            <span className="text-[11px] text-ant-mid/60">→</span>
   426	                                                                                        </>
   427	                                                                                    )}
   428	                                                                                    <span className="text-[11px] text-ant-ink font-semibold break-words">{diff.next || '—'}</span>
   429	                                                                                </span>
   430	                                                                            </div>
   431	                                                                        ))}
   432	                                                                    </div>
   433	                                                                )}
   434	
   435	                                                                <p className="text-[10.5px] text-ant-mid">
   436	                                                                    {author ? `Par ${author} · ` : ''}
   437	                                                                    {isValid(createdAt) ? format(createdAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '—'}
   438	                                                                    {resolvedAt && isValid(resolvedAt) ? ` · Mis à jour ${format(resolvedAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm')}` : ''}

   123	        const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

   125	        const res = await fetch(EDGE_FUNCTION_URL, {

   128	            body: JSON.stringify({ action, provider_id: providerId, data }),

   130	        const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as Record<string, unknown>));
   131	        if (!res.ok) {

   137	        }

   150	            return {

   157	        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,

   160

   182	                payload.package_address = address.trim();

   186	            const respPayload = ((json as { data?: Record<string, unknown> }).data ?? json) as Record<string, unknown>;
