import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { format, isValid } from 'date-fns';
import { Loader2, Truck, SearchX, Hash, Phone, MapPin, CalendarClock, Info, PencilLine } from 'lucide-react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
    Dialog,
    DialogContent,
    DialogDescription,
    DialogHeader,
    DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { matchPackageStatus } from '@/lib/matchPackageStatus';
import { trackParcelWithFallback, getCachedTracking } from '@/lib/orders/syncOrderTracking';
import { DynamicIcon } from '@/lib/renderReactIcon';

type ParcelTrackingDialogProps = {
    open: boolean;
    onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
    orderId: string | null;
};

type DataListItem = {
    title: string;
    value: string | number;
    open_url?: string;
};

type PkgStatus = {
    id: string;
    code: string;
    label: string;
    color: string | null;
    icon: string | null;
    delivery_service_statuses: string[] | null;
};

/** A parcel-change demand we filed with the provider (public.parcel_change_demands). */
type ChangeDemand = {
    id: string;
    ref: string;
    request_type: string;
    status: string;
    old_values: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    new_values: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    created_at: string;
    resolved_at: string | null;
    created_by_user?: { name?: string | null } | { name?: string | null }[] | null;
};

const DEMAND_TYPE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
    CORRECT_INFORMATION: "Correction d'informations",
    CHANGE_DESTINATION: 'Changement de destination',
};

const DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
    PENDING: { label: 'En attente', className: 'border border-ant-line text-ant-mid' },
    APPROVED: { label: 'Approuvée', className: 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green' },
    REJECTED: { label: 'Refusée', className: 'bg-ant-rose/10 text-ant-rose' },
    UNKNOWN: { label: 'Mise à jour reçue', className: 'bg-ant-ink/5 text-ant-mid' },
};

/** Demand payload keys, in display order (written by the provider-integration edge fn). */
const DEMAND_FIELDS: { key: string; label: string }[] = [
    { key: 'phone', label: 'Téléphone' },
    { key: 'receiver', label: 'Destinataire' },
    { key: 'price', label: 'Prix' },
    { key: 'note', label: 'Note' },
    { key: 'city', label: 'Ville' },
    { key: 'address', label: 'Adresse' },
];

const demandText = (v: unknown): string => {
    if (v === null || v === undefined) return '';
    if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'Oui' : 'Non';
    if (typeof v === 'object') return '';
    return String(v).trim();
};

/** Only the fields that actually changed — skip equal, both-empty, or absent-in-new. */
const demandDiffs = (d: ChangeDemand) => {
    const oldV = (d.old_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const newV = (d.new_values || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    return DEMAND_FIELDS.flatMap(({ key, label }) => {
        if (newV[key] === undefined) return [];
        const next = demandText(newV[key]);
        const prev = demandText(oldV[key]);
        if (!next && !prev) return [];
        // Numeric equality for the price only — a phone like 0612… must never be
        // compared as a number (leading zero would make it equal to 612…).
        const numeric = key === 'price' && prev !== '' && next !== ''
            && Number.isFinite(Number(prev)) && Number.isFinite(Number(next));
        if (numeric ? Number(prev) === Number(next) : prev === next) return [];
        return [{ key, label, prev, next }];
    });
};

const demandAuthor = (d: ChangeDemand): string => {
    const u = d.created_by_user;
    const row = Array.isArray(u) ? u[0] : u;
    return (row?.name || '').trim();
};

export function ParcelTrackingDialog({ open, onOpenChange, orderId }: ParcelTrackingDialogProps) {
    const { toast } = useToast();
    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    const [trackingCode, setTrackingCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [trackingHistory, setTrackingHistory] = useState<unknown>(null);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

    // Load all package_statuses for icon/color matching
    const { data: pkgStatuses } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['package-statuses-icons'],
        queryFn: async () => {
            const { data } = await supabase
                .from('package_statuses')
                .select('id, code, label, color, icon, delivery_service_statuses')
                .eq('active', true)
                .eq('show_in_courier', true);
            return (data || []) as unknown as PkgStatus[];
        },
        // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — without this gate the query fired on every
        // page load of every host page with the dialog closed.
        enabled: open,
        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
    });

    // Parcel-change demands we filed for this parcel. Additive + silent: on error
    // the timeline renders exactly as it does today.
    const { data: demands } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['parcel-change-demands', trackingCode],
        queryFn: async () => {
            const { data } = await (supabase as any)
                .from('parcel_change_demands')
                .select('id, ref, request_type, status, old_values, new_values, created_at, resolved_at, created_by_user:users!parcel_change_demands_created_by_fkey(name)')
                .eq('tracking_code', trackingCode as string)
                .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
            return (data || []) as ChangeDemand[];
        },
        // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — gate on `open` (see the comment above).
        enabled: open && !!trackingCode,
        staleTime: 60 * 1000,
    });

    const hasApprovedDemand = (demands ?? []).some(d => d.status === 'APPROVED');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open || !orderId) {
            setTrackingCode(null);
            setTrackingHistory(null);
            setIsLoading(false);
            return;
        }

        let cancelled = false;

        const run = async () => {
            setIsLoading(true);
            setTrackingHistory(null);
            setTrackingCode(null);

            try {
                const { data: parcel, error } = await supabase
                    .from('company_parcels')
                    .select('id, tracking_code, external_id, provider_id, provider:providers(id, name, provider_type, logo_url)')
                    .eq('order_id', orderId)
                    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
                    .limit(1)
                    .maybeSingle();

                if (cancelled) return;
                if (error) throw error;

                if (!parcel?.tracking_code || !String(parcel.tracking_code).trim() || !parcel.provider_id) {
                    const { data: courierParcel, error: courierErr } = await (supabase as any)
                        .from('courier_parcels')
                        .select('id, tracking_code, status_id, created_at, courier_id, status:package_statuses(label, color), courier:users!courier_parcels_courier_id_fkey(id, name, phone)')
                        .eq('order_id', orderId)
                        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
                        .limit(1)
                        .maybeSingle();
                    if (cancelled) return;
                    if (courierErr) throw courierErr;

                    if (!courierParcel?.tracking_code || !String(courierParcel.tracking_code).trim()) {
                        toast({ title: 'Pas de suivi', description: 'Aucun colis avec code de suivi pour cette commande.', variant: 'destructive' });
                        onOpenChange(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    setTrackingCode(String(courierParcel.tracking_code).trim());
                    const courierName = courierParcel.courier?.name || 'Coursier';
                    const courierPhone = courierParcel.courier?.phone || '';
                    const cpLabel = courierParcel.status?.label || 'Nouveau';
                    const cpColor = courierParcel.status?.color || '#3b82f6';
                    setTrackingHistory({
                        statusLabel: cpLabel,
                        statusColor: cpColor,
                        history: [{
                            status: cpLabel,
                            statusLabel: cpLabel,
                            date: courierParcel.created_at || null,
                            comment: 'Colis créé en interne et confié à ' + courierName,
                            color: cpColor,
                            user: courierName,
                            phone: courierPhone,
                        }],
                    });
                    // D1 keys — the pre-D1 ['orders'] key matches nothing anymore (OR-9)
                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-page'] });
                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-kpis'] });
                    return;
                }

                setTrackingCode(String(parcel.tracking_code).trim());

                // Throttle (owner-approved 2026-07-17): a parcel refreshed in
                // the last 10min replays its cached result — no external
                // the last 10min replays its cached result — no external
                // carrier call, no status re-write, no global invalidations.
                const cached = getCachedTracking(String(parcel.tracking_code).trim());
                if (cached) {
                    setTrackingHistory(cached);
                    return;
                }

                const data = await trackParcelWithFallback({
                    id: parcel.id,
                    provider_id: parcel.provider_id,
                    tracking_code: String(parcel.tracking_code).trim(),
                    external_id: parcel.external_id,
                });

                if (cancelled) return;
                setTrackingHistory(data);

                const providerStatus = (data.statusLabel || data.status || data.current_status) as string | undefined;

                if (providerStatus) {
                    const matched = await matchPackageStatus(supabase, providerStatus);
                    // status_id is the only persisted parcel status; skip when unmatched
                    // rather than wiping the existing status.
                    if (matched?.id) {
                        await supabase
                            .from('company_parcels')
                            .update({ status_id: matched.id })
                            .eq('id', parcel.id);
                    }
                }
                // D1 keys — the pre-D1 ['orders'] key matches nothing anymore (OR-9)
                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-page'] });
                    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders-kpis'] });
            } catch (e: unknown) {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    toast({ title: 'Tracking Error', description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Échec du suivi', variant: 'destructive' });
                    onOpenChange(false);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) setIsLoading(false);
            }
        };

        run();
        return () => { cancelled = true; };
    }, [open, orderId]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

    const statusMap: Record<string, string> = {
        NEW_PARCEL: 'Nouveau', WAITING_PICKUP: 'En attente de ramassage', DISTRIBUTION: 'En cours de distribution',
        DELIVERED: 'Livré', REFUSED: 'Refusé', REFUSE: 'Refusé', ANNULE: 'Annulé', CANCELLED: 'Annulé',
        RETURNED: 'Retourné', RETOUR: 'Retourné', POSTPONED: 'Reporté', WAITING_PREPARATION: 'En préparation',
        PICKED_UP: 'Ramassé', READY_FOR_PICKUP: 'Prêt pour ramassage', SENT: 'Expédié', RECEIVED: 'Reçu',
        SCHEDULED: 'Programmé', PROGRAMMER: 'Programmé', NO_ANSWER: 'Pas de réponse', RELAUNCH: 'Relancer',
    };

    const situationMap: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
        PAID: { label: 'Payé', className: 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green border-ant-green/30' },
        NOT_PAID: { label: 'Non payé', className: 'bg-ant-orange/10 text-ant-orange border-ant-orange/30' },
        INVOICED: { label: 'Facturé', className: 'bg-ant-blue/10 text-ant-blue border-blue-200' },
    };

    /** Match an event's raw status string to a package_statuses row for icon/color */
    const matchPkgStatus = (rawStatus: string, statusLabel?: string): PkgStatus | null => {
        if (!pkgStatuses?.length) return null;
        const candidates = [statusLabel, rawStatus].filter(Boolean) as string[];
        for (const candidate of candidates) {
            const lower = candidate.toLowerCase();
            // Match by delivery_service_statuses array
            const byDss = pkgStatuses.find(ps =>
                ps.delivery_service_statuses?.some(dss => dss.toLowerCase() === lower)
            );
            if (byDss) return byDss;
            // Match by label
            const byLabel = pkgStatuses.find(ps => ps.label.toLowerCase() === lower);
            if (byLabel) return byLabel;
            // Match by code
            const byCode = pkgStatuses.find(ps => ps.code.toLowerCase() === lower);
            if (byCode) return byCode;
        }
        return null;
    };

    return (
        <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
            <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[560px] rounded-[32px] border-none p-0 overflow-hidden bg-white">
                <DialogHeader className="p-8 pb-0">
                    <DialogTitle className="text-2xl font-black text-ant-ink flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="p-2 bg-ant-ink/5 rounded-xl">
                            <Truck className="w-5 h-5 text-ant-ink" />
                        </div>
                        Suivi du colis
                    </DialogTitle>
                    <DialogDescription className="font-bold text-ant-muted">
                        Code: <span className="text-ant-ink">{trackingCode || '—'}</span>
                        {hasApprovedDemand && (
                            <span className="ml-2 align-middle inline-flex items-center text-[10px] font-semibold bg-ant-ink/5 text-ant-ink rounded-md px-2 py-0.5">
                                Infos modifiées
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </DialogDescription>
                </DialogHeader>

                <div className="p-8 max-h-[65vh] overflow-y-auto">
                    {isLoading ? (
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 gap-4">
                            <Loader2 className="w-10 h-10 text-ant-ink animate-spin" />
                            <p className="font-bold text-ant-muted animate-pulse">Récupération des données...</p>
                        </div>
                    ) : trackingHistory ? (
                        <div className="relative">
                            {(() => {
                                const th = trackingHistory as Record<string, unknown> | unknown[] | null;
                                let events: unknown[] = Array.isArray(th)
                                    ? th
                                    : Array.isArray((th as Record<string, unknown>)?.history)
                                      ? ((th as Record<string, unknown>).history as unknown[])
                                      : [];
                                if (events.length === 0 && th && !Array.isArray(th)) {
                                    const o = th as Record<string, unknown>;
                                    events = [{
                                        status: o.statusLabel || o.status || 'Nouveau',
                                        date: null,
                                        comment: '',
                                        statusLabel: o.statusLabel,
                                        color: o.statusColor || o.color,
                                    }];
                                }

                                // Reverse: oldest first (top) → newest last (bottom)
                                const sorted = [...events].reverse();

                                // Interleave the change demands by created_at. Events keep
                                // their relative order; an event with no parseable date
                                // inherits the last known timestamp so it stays anchored
                                // next to its neighbours.
                                type TimelineItem =
                                    | { kind: 'event'; ts: number; ord: number; event: Record<string, unknown> }
                                    | { kind: 'demand'; ts: number; ord: number; demand: ChangeDemand };
                                let carried = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
                                const eventItems: TimelineItem[] = sorted.map((raw, i) => {
                                    const ev = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
                                    const d = ev.date ? new Date(ev.date as string) : null;
                                    if (d && isValid(d)) carried = d.getTime();
                                    return { kind: 'event', ts: carried, ord: i, event: ev };
                                });
                                const demandItems: TimelineItem[] = (demands ?? []).map((dm, i) => {
                                    const d = new Date(dm.created_at);
                                    return {
                                        kind: 'demand',
                                        ts: isValid(d) ? d.getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
                                        ord: sorted.length + i,
                                        demand: dm,
                                    };
                                });
                                const merged = [...eventItems, ...demandItems].sort((a, b) =>
                                    a.ts === b.ts ? a.ord - b.ord : a.ts < b.ts ? -1 : 1,
                                );
                                const lastEventOrd = sorted.length - 1;

                                return (
                                    <div className="relative pl-10">
                                        {/* Vertical line */}
                                        <div className="absolute left-[15px] top-4 bottom-4 w-0.5 bg-gradient-to-b from-ant-line via-ant-line to-ant-ink/30" />

                                        <div className="space-y-0">
                                            {merged.map((item: TimelineItem, idx: number) => {
                                                if (item.kind === 'demand') {
                                                    const dm = item.demand;
                                                    const chip = DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP[dm.status] || DEMAND_STATUS_CHIP.UNKNOWN;
                                                    const diffs = demandDiffs(dm);
                                                    const author = demandAuthor(dm);

                                                    return (

                                                            {/* Timeline node */}
                                                            <div className="absolute -left-10 top-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-[3px] border-white bg-ant-light text-ant-ink shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center z-10">
                                                                {dm.request_type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' ? (
                                                                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />

                                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">

                                                                    </span>
                                                                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${chip.className}`}>
                                                                        {chip.label}
                                                                    </span>
                                                                    <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] tabular-nums text-ant-mid">{dm.ref}</span>

     1	-- Parcel change demands (TKT-8 follow-up): local source of truth for the
     2	-- Coliaty parcel-change demands WE file. Created by the provider-integration
     3	-- edge fn (service role) at demand creation; reconciled fail-open by the
     4	-- provider-webhook when a payload referencing the demand ref arrives.
     5	-- Coliaty documents no demand webhook, so status/raw stay tolerant.
     6	
     7	BEGIN;
     8	
     9	CREATE TABLE public.parcel_change_demands (
    10	    id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    11	    "DigiCom Business" uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.businesses(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    12	    order_id        uuid REFERENCES public.orders(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    13	    provider_id     uuid REFERENCES public.providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    14	    tracking_code   text NOT NULL,
    15	    ref             text NOT NULL,
    16	    request_type    text NOT NULL CHECK (request_type IN ('CORRECT_INFORMATION', 'CHANGE_DESTINATION')),
    17	    -- PENDING at creation; APPROVED/REJECTED when a webhook lets us conclude;
    18	    -- UNKNOWN when a webhook referenced the demand without a recognizable verdict.
    19	    status          text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'UNKNOWN')),
    20	    provider_status_raw text,
    21	    -- Snapshots at file time: old = order/parcel values then, new = requested.
    22	    old_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    23	    new_values      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    24	    raw             jsonb,
    25	    created_by      uuid REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    26	    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    27	    resolved_at     timestamptz,
    28	    webhook_log_id  uuid REFERENCES public.webhook_logs(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
    29	);
    30	
    31	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX parcel_change_demands_ref_key ON public.parcel_change_demands (ref);
    32	CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_tracking_code ON public.parcel_change_demands (tracking_code);
    33	CREATE INDEX idx_pcd_biz_created ON public.parcel_change_demands ("DigiCom Business", created_at DESC);

    38	-- Tenant read-only: members see their business's demands. All writes go through
    39	-- the service role (edge fns) — no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy for users.
    40	CREATE POLICY pcd_tenant_select ON public.parcel_change_demands

-- ============================================================================
-- RLS tenant-scoping: status pipeline + users/businesses PII  (Security batch 1)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Closes C1/C2 from the 2026-06 pipeline audit:
--   * Any authenticated user of ANY tenant could read/write EVERY tenant's
--     orders, company_parcels, courier_parcels, webhook_logs and
--     provider_webhooks (the last exposing webhook_secret -> webhook forgery).
--   * public.users SELECT ("Profiles are viewable by everyone") and
--     public.businesses SELECT ("Allow authenticated read") leaked cross-tenant
--     PII (bank RIB, CIN, salary, ICE/RC fiscal IDs) to any authenticated user
--     (users was readable by anon too).
--
-- Model: strict per-tenant via public.current_user_digicom_business_id().
-- Owners (role='owner', "DigiCom Business" IS NULL until they pick a business)
-- intentionally see nothing on the 5 status tables until selection (confirmed
-- with product owner). Owners retain cross-tenant visibility ONLY where the
-- product needs it: the business picker and owner consoles (is_owner()).
--
-- Verified on prod before authoring (2026-06-02):
--   * service_role.rolbypassrls = true  -> ALL edge-function ingestion
--     (provider-webhook, *-poll-webhook, provider-integration, ai-review-act)
--     bypasses RLS and is UNAFFECTED by these TO authenticated policies.
--   * authenticated / anon do NOT bypass RLS -> these policies govern them.
--   * 0 NULL "DigiCom Business" rows on orders / company_parcels /
--     courier_parcels / webhook_logs / provider_webhooks / businesses
--     -> equality predicate orphans no legitimate row.
--   * Helpers re-declared CREATE OR REPLACE (identical to live bodies) so the
--     migration is self-contained and applies cleanly on a fresh branch.
--
-- Scope guard: this migration ONLY swaps the OPEN SELECT / ALL visibility
-- policies. The already role-gated WRITE policies on users (check_user_role)
-- and businesses (creator_id / is_owner) are LEFT INTACT.
-- NOTE: webhook_secret column-level REVOKE is intentionally NOT done here — it
-- must ship together with the Webhooks.tsx narrowed-select to avoid 500ing the
-- admin page (tracked as a separate coordinated change).
-- ============================================================================

-- 1) Tenant + owner helpers (self-contained; bodies identical to live) --------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
RETURNS uuid
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $$
  SELECT u."DigiCom Business"
  FROM public.users u
  WHERE u.id = auth.uid()
  LIMIT 1;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.current_user_digicom_business_id() FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.current_user_digicom_business_id() TO authenticated, service_role;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_owner()
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_catalog'
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid() AND role = 'owner'
  );
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_owner() FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_owner() TO authenticated, service_role;

-- 2) orders: 4 open per-command policies -> tenant-scoped ---------------------
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow authenticated users to read orders"   ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow authenticated users to insert orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow authenticated users to update orders" ON public.orders;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow authenticated users to delete orders" ON public.orders;

CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_select ON public.orders FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());
CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_insert ON public.orders FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());
CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_update ON public.orders FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());
CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_delete ON public.orders FOR DELETE TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());

-- 3) company_parcels / courier_parcels / provider_webhooks / webhook_logs -----
--    (each had a single open ALL policy -> single tenant-scoped ALL policy)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Authenticated users can manage company parcels" ON public.company_parcels;
CREATE POLICY company_parcels_tenant_all ON public.company_parcels FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Authenticated users can manage courier parcels" ON public.courier_parcels;
CREATE POLICY courier_parcels_tenant_all ON public.courier_parcels FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Enable all for authenticated users" ON public.provider_webhooks;
CREATE POLICY provider_webhooks_tenant_all ON public.provider_webhooks FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Enable all for authenticated users" ON public.webhook_logs;
CREATE POLICY webhook_logs_tenant_all ON public.webhook_logs FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());

-- 4) users SELECT: same-tenant + self + owner (writes already role-gated) -----
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Profiles are viewable by everyone" ON public.users;
CREATE POLICY users_tenant_select ON public.users FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    id = auth.uid()
    OR "DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
    OR public.is_owner()
    </>
  );
};

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    // retry 3 (the library default) burns 4 round-trips + ~7s of exponential
    // backoff on every DETERMINISTIC failure — an RLS 42501 (the admin-gated
    // stats RPCs), a PostgREST 400, a raised RPC guard. None of those get
    // better by retrying. Matches waQueryClient's policy.
    // staleTime is deliberately left at the library default (0): the heavy
    // pages all set their own 60s inline, while the un-swept pages have no
    // realtime and rely on focus-refetch to stay correct (see docs/d7-recon).
    queries: { retry: 1 },
    mutations: { retry: 0 },
  },
});

// The SPA never unmounts on logout (sign-out is a client-side navigate, there
// is no reload anywhere) and this client is a module singleton — so without
// this, signing in as another user renders the PREVIOUS user's cached rows
// stale-while-revalidating, and any query key that does not carry the business
// id (profiles, 'plans', 'zones', 'master-cities', 'businesses', …) can keep
// serving them. Purge everything on sign-out. Mirrors waQueryClient.
supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
  if (event === "SIGNED_OUT") queryClient.clear();
});

const App = () => (
  <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    <TooltipProvider delayDuration={150} skipDelayDuration={150}>
      <Toaster />
      <Sonner duration={500} />
      <BrowserRouter>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/auth" element={<Auth />} />
          {/* Public self-registration via a single-use admin invite link */}
          <Route path="/register/:token" element={<Register />} />
          {/* Public credential change (email/password) via a single-use admin-sent link */}
          <Route path="/account/change/:token" element={<ChangeCredentials />} />
          {/* Owner Routes */}

# TICKET E2 — demand persistence (provider-integration) + tolerant webhook reconciliation (provider-webhook)

## TASK
In repo `/Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast` (quote the path — spaces), wire the new `public.parcel_change_demands` table (migration `supabase/migrations/20260729100000_parcel_change_demands.sql` — READ IT FIRST for exact columns) into two edge functions.

## PART 1 — `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts`: record the demand at creation
In the `create_parcel_change_demand` dispatch case, after the adapter call succeeds:
1. The existing `assertParcelChangeAccess(req, supabase, providerId, trackingCode)` helper (just above `Deno.serve`) already resolves the caller and the parcel. Extend it minimally to RETURN `{ userId, businessId, parcelOrderId }` — add `order_id` to its `company_parcels` select. Do not change its checks or error behavior.
2. After a successful `adapter.createParcelChangeDemand(...)`, INSERT one row into `parcel_change_demands` using the service-role client:
   - `"DigiCom Business"` = businessId, `order_id` = parcelOrderId, `provider_id`, `tracking_code`,
   - `ref` = the demand ref from the adapter result, `request_type` = data.request_type, `status` = 'PENDING' (default),
   - `old_values` = snapshot fetched server-side from the `orders` row (`phone`, `name` → key `receiver`, `price`, `city`, `address` — check the actual orders columns in `src/lib/database.types.ts` or an existing select in this file; use only columns that exist; `note: null`),
   - `new_values` = the submitted fields `{ phone, receiver (from package_reciever), price, note, city (only CHANGE_DESTINATION), address (only CHANGE_DESTINATION) }`,
   - `raw` = the adapter's returned demand object, `created_by` = userId.
   Use `.upsert(..., { onConflict: 'ref' })` semantics OR a plain insert with a caught unique-violation — a Coliaty retry must not 500 the response.
3. **FAIL-OPEN:** the insert lives in its own try/catch; on failure `console.error` and still return the normal success response (the demand exists at Coliaty regardless).

## PART 2 — `supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts`: tolerant reconciliation branch
Coliaty documents NO demand webhook — we cannot know the payload shape. Add a defensive branch:
1. Slot: after the event-name extraction (`const eventName = String(payload.EVENT || '')...`, ~line 203-210) and BEFORE status-candidate assembly (~line 222). The webhook_logs insert (~133-139) and parcel/business resolution (~157-168) happen earlier — the branch can use `logEntry` and `businessId`.
2. Detection: stringify the payload once; collect all refs matching `/PCD-[0-9][0-9-]*/g` (case-insensitive). Also treat `eventName` containing `DEMAND` or `CHANGE_DEMAND` as a demand signal even without a ref (in that case just mark the log processed with a note — nothing to reconcile).
3. For each found ref, UPDATE `parcel_change_demands` WHERE `ref` = that ref AND `"DigiCom Business"` = businessId:
   - Verdict scan (payload string, lowercase): contains `approuv`/`approved`/`accept` → `status='APPROVED'`; contains `refus`/`reject`/`denied` → `status='REJECTED'`; otherwise set `status='UNKNOWN'` **only if the current status is 'PENDING'** (never downgrade a concluded status — use a conditional `.eq('status','PENDING')` on the no-verdict update).
   - Always set `provider_status_raw` = a short extracted status-ish string if one exists (payload.STATUS/STATUT/SITUATION/data.status — first non-empty) else the eventName, `resolved_at = now()`, `webhook_log_id = logEntry.id`.
4. **Flow control:** the whole branch is FAIL-OPEN (try/catch, log, continue). After reconciliation: if the payload yields NO status candidates for the normal pipeline (inspect how candidates are assembled ~222-234 — reuse the same extraction), mark the webhook log processed with message `Demand reconciliation only` and `return json({status:'demand_reconciled', refs:[...]})` — this prevents a status-less demand webhook from polluting the unmatched-status queue (TKT-6). If status candidates DO exist, fall through to the normal status pipeline unchanged.

## MUST DO
- Read the migration file first; column names are exact (`"DigiCom Business"` quoted).
- Keep both functions' existing behavior byte-identical for payloads with no PCD ref and no DEMAND event name.
- Report exact line ranges changed in both files.

## MUST NOT
- Touch any other file. No `deno check` (verifier runs it). No refactors. No writes to `orders` in either branch. Do not spawn subagents.

## WRITE SET
`supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts`.

## OUTPUT FORMAT
First line `DONE`/`DONE_WITH_CONCERNS`/`NEEDS_CONTEXT`/`BLOCKED`; per-part changes with file:line; concerns.

## ORIGINAL TASK
(1) provider-integration `create_parcel_change_demand`: after Coliaty success, insert a `parcel_change_demands` row (business/order/provider/tracking_code/ref/request_type/old_values snapshot from the orders row/new_values from the submission/raw/created_by) — FAIL-OPEN (insert failure must not fail the API response); retry-safe on duplicate ref. (2) provider-webhook: tolerant branch after event-name extraction — find `PCD-…` refs anywhere in the payload; update matching demand rows scoped to the webhook's business (verdict scan: approuv/approved/accept → APPROVED, refus/reject → REJECTED, else UNKNOWN only-if-PENDING; always provider_status_raw + resolved_at + webhook_log_id); FAIL-OPEN; if the payload has no status candidates for the normal pipeline, mark log processed and return early (`demand_reconciled`) so status-less demand webhooks don't pollute the unmatched-status queue; with candidates present, normal flow must be untouched.

## CRITERIA (cite file:line each)
1. Insert fires only on adapter success, carries every column listed above with names matching the migration exactly (`"DigiCom Business"` quoted), and is wrapped so its failure cannot alter the success response (trace the return path).
2. Duplicate-ref retry cannot 500 (upsert on ref, or unique-violation caught).
3. `assertParcelChangeAccess` still performs the same 401/403 checks; its extension only ADDS returned data.
4. Webhook branch: regex actually matches `PCD-1780429520-8131`-style refs; updates are scoped `.eq('"DigiCom Business"', …)` or equivalent; the no-verdict update is conditioned on current status PENDING (cannot downgrade APPROVED); resolved_at/webhook_log_id/provider_status_raw set on every reconciliation.
5. Early-return happens ONLY when (refs-or-demand-event detected) AND (zero status candidates by the same extraction the normal pipeline uses); payloads with candidates fall through to an UNCHANGED status pipeline (diff the surrounding code paths).
6. A payload with no PCD ref and no DEMAND event name takes a byte-identical path to before (read the diff hunks: nothing else touched).
7. No writes to `orders` in the new code.
8. `deno check` both files from repo root; then `git restore deno.lock`; confirm `git status --porcelain | grep deno.lock` empty.
9. Write set: within `supabase/`, only the two named files + the migration file changed (`git status --porcelain -- supabase/`).

Edit source (deno.lock restore excepted); spawn subagents.

Show parcel-change demands in the tracking modal with a premium minimal design (better than Coliaty's red/green blocks): one card per demand interleaved in the timeline at created_at, monochrome old→new field diffs (old struck-through ant-mid, new ant-ink), status chip (En attente/Approuvée/Refusée/Mise à jour reçue), PCD ref, filed-by + dates, an « Infos modifiées » header chip when an APPROVED demand exists, and invalidation of the demands query when a new demand is filed from ParcelChangeDemandDialog.

## CRITERIA (cite file:line each)
1. Demands query exists, keyed `['parcel-change-demands', <tracking code>]`, `enabled` gated on the dialog's `open` (kept-mounted trap), reads `parcel_change_demands` by tracking_code with columns that exist in the migration; creator-name embed is defensive (UI renders when it's null).
2. Demand cards interleave with tracking events by date consistently with the existing ordering logic (verify the merge/sort actually positions by created_at relative to event dates — construct the comparison mentally from the code, cite it).
3. Diff rows render ONLY genuinely changed fields (equal / both-empty / undefined-new skipped — check the comparison); old value uses line-through + muted token, new value ink+semibold; NO red/green diff coloring; no side-stripe borders (`border-l-*` accent) anywhere in the new JSX.
4. Status chip covers all four statuses incl. UNKNOWN; ref rendered; footer has Par/date and conditional « Mis à jour ».
5. « Infos modifiées » chip appears only when ≥1 demand is APPROVED.
6. A failing/empty demands query leaves the timeline exactly as before (silent additive).
7. ParcelChangeDemandDialog invalidates `['parcel-change-demands', …]` with a key that MATCHES criterion 1's key shape (same tracking-code value source) on success.

-- SECURITY RLS SWEEP — PHASE 5: revoke the blanket `anon` table grants (defense-in-depth).
--
-- The Supabase clone default granted `anon` FULL table+column DML
-- (SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER) on EVERY public table — including
-- UPDATE on users.role and users.salary_amount. After Phases 1-4 the RLS is tenant-scoped on
-- every table and is the live gate that blocks anon, so these grants are inert today — but they
-- are a privilege-escalation landmine if any policy ever regresses or a table's RLS is disabled.
--
-- anon legitimately needs exactly ONE thing over PostgREST: SELECT(email, username) on `users`
-- for the username->email login lookup (Auth.tsx:122). Register + ChangeCredentials authenticate
-- via GoTrue + service_role edge functions and never read tables as anon (verified empirically:
-- post-Phase-4, anon could already only read `users`; businesses/plans/platform_settings/
-- user_invitations all returned 0 under RLS).
--
-- `REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES` clears BOTH table-level and column-level grants
-- (verified on dev: 0 table + 0 column anon grants remain), so we then re-grant only the two
-- login-lookup columns. authenticated/service_role grants are untouched.
--
-- Rollback: GRANT ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO anon; (do NOT — restores the landmine).

revoke all privileges on all tables in schema public from anon;
grant select (email, username) on public.users to anon;
# 11 — `whatsapp` Schema Build Plan

**Status:** LOCKED PLAN v3 — from the final live sweep of the WA Mancer managed DB (2026-07-05, project `xlhlezgpgrypdijynmqr`, PG 17.6), a 4-lens adversarial verification pass, and two owner rulings (execution target = box; **keep the logs/telemetry stack, drop only identity**). This is the DDL-level port map for the new `whatsapp` schema on the live box.

**Meta-rule:** WA Mancer is the exact spec, not inspiration. The port is **near-total 1:1**. Only THREE kinds of change are allowed:
1. **Schema rename** `public.*` → `whatsapp.*` (mechanical).
2. **Drop the IDENTITY layer only** — iGrowFast already owns auth/users/roles/invitations; keeping WA Mancer's is dead weight (§2.1).
3. **Add the two adaptation layers** (multi-tenant/roles/owner + e-commerce seam, §4) and apply **security-grant hardening** (§5 — closes grant holes, removes no feature).

**Everything else — including the ENTIRE logs / statistics / telemetry / audit stack — ports verbatim.** What to prune from that stack is a SEPARATE later editing pass, not a build-time decision. Do not drop it now.

---

## 0. Execution target — RESOLVED 2026-07-05 (owner ruling)

**Live prod = the self-hosted box `.102`. The managed Supabase projects are LEGACY — never touch them for this build.**

- **Live target:** Contabo box **207.180.232.102**, self-host Supabase (`supabase-db` on `igf/supabase-postgres:17.6.1.136-pgbackrest.1` + auth / edge / storage / realtime / meta / supavisor pooler / postgrest v14.12 / kong / cloudflared). Verified: `whatsapp` schema absent (clean), 17 live app crons, 5 e-comm anchor tables present.
- **LEGACY — never receive `whatsapp` DDL:** `mcp__supabase-igrowfast-prod` → `zjkqprrfbibjoslvlmxf` (AWS managed) and `mcp__supabase-igrowfast-dev` → `pcgvfwqkzyozzxktfogi`. The whatsappmancer MCP is read-only reference.
- **Reach the box (no MCP hits it):** `ssh igf` → `docker exec supabase-db psql -U postgres -d postgres`. Every wave and every fact check runs this way. The Supabase MCP `apply_migration`/`execute_sql` tools CANNOT be used.
- **Rehearsal gap (§7):** no live dev *mirror of the box* exists (legacy dev is a clone of old managed prod). Box-native substitute: rehearse each wave in a throwaway `whatsapp_rehearse` scratch schema (or `BEGIN … ROLLBACK`) on the box, run advisor-equivalent + impersonation checks, snapshot, then apply for real. Owner to confirm (F8).

## 1. Target and naming rules

- **Where:** the live box Postgres (§0), new schema **`whatsapp`**. WA Mancer's own DB is untouched (separate live product).
- **Object names:** IDENTICAL to WA Mancer — only the schema qualifier changes, so ported worker/edge/UI SQL diffs stay minimal. Enum types also live in `whatsapp`.
- **PostgREST exposure:** add `whatsapp` to **`PGRST_DB_SCHEMAS`** on the **`supabase-rest`** container (currently `public,storage,graphql_public`) + restart/reload. **Kong does NOT own the exposed-schema list.** Clients use `.schema('whatsapp')`.

## 2. Scope

### 2.1 The ONLY drop: WA Mancer's identity layer (iGrowFast owns it)
iGrowFast already has `public.users` (its `profiles` equivalent), a 6-value `public.user_role`, per-business role helpers, and its own invitation/onboarding lifecycle `[user-mgmt-uniqueness-model, invitation-email-send]`. Keeping WA Mancer's parallel identity would fork auth and create months of reconciliation. **Drop and rewire:**
- Tables: `profiles`, `invitations`.
- Enum: `user_role`.
- Functions: `is_admin()`, `has_role()`, `bootstrap_admin`, `admin_set_user_role`, `admin_set_user_active`, `invitation_claim/finalize/rollback/status`, `validate_avatar_config`, `_on_auth_user_created`, `_on_profile_role_changed`, `_on_invitation_inserted`, `_on_invitation_revoked`.
- Triggers: the 5 on `profiles`/`invitations`.
- **Rewire (L1, §4.1):** every RLS predicate + RPC guard that used `is_admin()`/`profiles` → iGrowFast per-business role helpers on `public.users`; `conversation_assignments.user_id` → iGrowFast `auth.users` (sellers). `_release_dms_on_profile_deactivation` re-hung on `public.users`. ~30 `is_admin()` call sites get rewritten — that IS the L1 work.

### 2.2 KEEP the entire logs / statistics / telemetry / audit stack — port 1:1, prune LATER
**Owner ruling 2026-07-05:** do NOT drop the observability stack at build time. Port it verbatim; a separate later editing pass decides what to keep vs prune. In scope to KEEP:
- Tables: `audit_log` (+ hash chain), `system_logs`, `metric_samples`, `host_metrics`, `db_metrics_history`, `worker_health_history`, `r2_metrics`, `app_cache`, `og_cache`, `_cron_ingest_state`, `dispatcher_snapshot`, `worker_health`.
- Functions: `audit()`, `_audit_log_hash`, `validate_audit_chain`, `admin_audit_chain_status`, `admin_query_logs`, `admin_log_facets`, all `stats_*`(+`_impl`), `monitor_*`, `db_metrics_*`, `ingest_host/prom/r2_metrics`, `ingest_system_logs`, `ingest_platform_logs`, `cache_get_or_compute`, `cache_warm/janitor/quantize`, `dashboard_overview`, `agent_dashboard_overview`, `agent_performance`, `dashboard_activity_impl`, `instance_stats`(+`_impl`), and the `private._compute_*` layer.
- Enums: `log_source` (14 values), `log_level`.
- Crons: all 16, incl. the 9 telemetry/metric/log/cache jobs.
- Realtime: all 14 published tables.

This keeps `audit()` (called by ~25 operational RPCs) and every stats/dashboard function intact → **no dangling dependencies**, and the admin Statistics/Dashboard + owner Log/Monitor pages keep their exact WA Mancer data sources. The `_cron_purge_metric_samples` PUBLIC-execute grant is the one thing normalized (safety, §5 W3), not dropped.

### 2.3 Inventory the sweep found (context)
35 tables, 3 `security_invoker` views, 13 enums, 174 public + 22 private functions, 32 triggers, 58 RLS policies, 16 pg_cron jobs, 14 realtime tables, 0 storage buckets (media on R2), 1 vault secret. Full detail: sweep artifacts + `02-database.md`.

## 3. Port disposition

### 3.1 Tables (35 source → 35 in the new schema)

**PORT 1:1 (31)** — identical columns/defaults/constraints/indexes; only schema changes (+ §4 seam columns where flagged):
- **Inbox core:** `whatsapp_instances` (+`business_id` NOT NULL, +`node_id`, +`key_id`), `conversations` (+`customer_id`, +`key_id`), `messages` (+`key_id`), `conversation_assignments` (+ same-business guard, §4.1), `outbound_queue`, `number_pacing_state`, `worker_instance_leases`, `worker_health`, `dispatcher_snapshot`, `media_fetch_jobs`.
- **Contact bible:** `contacts` (+`customer_id`), `contact_identifiers`, `jid_mapping`, `merge_decisions`, `merge_review`, `contact_profiles`, `group_participants`.
- **Collab:** `tags`, `conversation_tags`, `conversation_notes`, `scheduled_messages`.
- **Logs/telemetry (KEEP, §2.2):** `audit_log`, `system_logs`, `metric_samples`, `host_metrics`, `db_metrics_history`, `worker_health_history`, `r2_metrics`, `app_cache`, `og_cache`, `_cron_ingest_state` (RLS enabled — W4 fix).

**ADAPT (2):**
- `quick_replies` → per-business: `instance_id` becomes `business_id`; RLS + unique index re-keyed.
- `message_templates` → per-business: +`business_id`; `'global'` = business-wide; CHECKs/partial-unique on `(business_id,name)`.

**DROP (2, identity only, §2.1):** `profiles`, `invitations`.

**ADD (2, seams, deny-all posture §3.10):** `encryption_keys` (key rotation), `wa_nodes` (node registry).

Net new schema = 31 PORT + 2 ADAPT + 2 ADD = **35 tables** + 3 views.

### 3.2 Enums (PORT 12 of 13)
DROP `user_role` only. PORT 12 verbatim incl. `log_source`/`log_level` (needed by `system_logs`, kept).

### 3.3 Views (3, `security_invoker=on`)
`messages_decrypted`, `conversations_decrypted`, `worker_health_status`. Grant `SELECT` to `authenticated` only — no anon, no DML (W2 fix).

### 3.4 Functions
**PORT everything** (inbox core, contact bible, queue/worker, crypto chain, agent/admin RPCs, AND the whole stats/monitor/ingest/cache/audit family §2.2). Only two adjustments:
- Normalize the 4 `search_path=public` stragglers to `''` (W6), bodies fully qualified.
- Rewrite the ~30 internal `is_admin()`/`has_role()` calls to the iGrowFast per-business helper (L1, §4.1). `audit()` stays as-is (no change).
**DROP** only the identity functions listed in §2.1.
> `private` schema collapses into `whatsapp`; the `_compute_*`/`cache_*` twins port too. Name collisions (`is_admin`/`has_role`/`audit`/`set_instance_token` in both source schemas) resolve by dropping the identity twins (`is_admin`/`has_role`) and keeping the operational ones.

### 3.5 Triggers (PORT 27, DROP 5)
DROP the 5 on `profiles`/`invitations`. **PORT the other 27 verbatim** — incl. the `_on_*` audit-enrichment triggers and `_audit_log_hash` (audit machinery stays with the logs stack). ADD the same-business assignment guard (§4.1). `_release_dms_on_profile_deactivation` re-hung on `public.users`.

### 3.6 pg_cron (PORT all 16)
All jobs port, renamed `wa_*` to coexist. Verify `cron.job` collisions — box uses ids 1–16, 20; `wa_*` names collision-free.

-- whatsapp_000_bootstrap  (Wave 0)
-- TARGET: LIVE box .102 self-host Postgres ONLY  (ssh igf -> docker exec supabase-db psql -U postgres).
-- NEVER apply to managed -prod (zjkqprrfbibjoslvlmxf) or -dev (pcgvfwqkzyozzxktfogi).
-- Applied 2026-07-05 (pre-apply PITR anchor: LSN 6/11002C48, 2026-07-05 19:36:55+00).
--
-- Wave 0 = create the empty `whatsapp` schema and lock it to authenticated+service_role.
--
-- Security model — EMPIRICALLY VERIFIED on this box 2026-07-05 (not assumed from the plan):
--   * anon gets NO schema USAGE -> cannot reach any whatsapp object regardless of object ACL.
--   * No all-schema/global default privileges exist and none are scoped to `whatsapp`; the plan's
--     W1 "grant-all trap" (auto-grant of new objects to anon/authenticated) is ABSENT here. New
--     whatsapp TABLES/SEQUENCES are created with owner-only ACLs.
--   * On this box `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES ... REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTIONS FROM PUBLIC` does NOT
--     take effect (new funcs keep the built-in PUBLIC=EXECUTE). It is therefore OMITTED (a no-op
--     would only create false confidence). Function PUBLIC-execute is instead stripped
--     deterministically PER FUNCTION in every function-creating wave:
--         REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION whatsapp.fn(args) FROM PUBLIC;
--         GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION whatsapp.fn(args) TO <intended roles only>;
--     (W5 grant==policy). Sensitive crypto fns (wave 2) -> EXECUTE to postgres+service_role only.

create schema if not exists whatsapp authorization postgres;
revoke all on schema whatsapp from public;                       -- no implicit PUBLIC usage/create
grant usage on schema whatsapp to authenticated, service_role;   -- anon gets NOTHING (S4/W2)

-- OUT-OF-BAND (not SQL): add `whatsapp` to PGRST_DB_SCHEMAS in /opt/supabase-project/.env and
-- `docker compose up -d --no-deps rest studio`.  See ../ops/wave0_rest_expose.sh.
docs/STATUS_F3_SYNC_RUNBOOK.md:67:- `pg_proc.prosrc ILIKE '%category%'` → empty in public schema.

hook: PreToolUse

-- TKT-6: capture provider webhook statuses that match nothing in the status book,
-- surface them for owner mapping, and apply mappings retroactively.
-- provider-webhook no-match branch: stamps company_parcels.raw_provider_status and
-- upserts the queue via log_unmatched_provider_status (service_role). Owner maps via
-- resolve_unmatched_provider_status: extends the master's delivery_service_statuses
-- list, backfills parcels sitting on that raw status (sync triggers cascade to
-- orders), and marks the queue row resolved.

-- 1. raw marker on provider parcels (planned in the 2026-06 status sweep, never landed)
alter table public.company_parcels
  add column if not exists raw_provider_status text;

create index if not exists idx_company_parcels_raw_status
  on public.company_parcels (lower(raw_provider_status))
  where raw_provider_status is not null;

-- 2. the queue
create table if not exists public.unmatched_provider_statuses (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  provider_type text not null,
  raw_status text not null,
  first_seen timestamptz not null default now(),
  last_seen timestamptz not null default now(),
  hit_count integer not null default 1,
  resolved_at timestamptz,
  resolved_status_id uuid references public.package_statuses(id),
  resolved_by uuid
);

create unique index if not exists unmatched_provider_statuses_key
  on public.unmatched_provider_statuses (provider_type, lower(raw_status));

alter table public.unmatched_provider_statuses enable row level security;

create policy "Owner all unmatched_provider_statuses"
  on public.unmatched_provider_statuses
  for all to authenticated
  using ((select is_owner()))
  with check ((select is_owner()));

grant select, insert, update, delete on public.unmatched_provider_statuses to authenticated;
revoke all on public.unmatched_provider_statuses from anon;

-- 3. webhook-side upsert (service_role only; atomic on the expression unique index)
create or replace function public.log_unmatched_provider_status(p_provider_type text, p_raw_status text)
returns void
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public, pg_temp
as $$
  insert into public.unmatched_provider_statuses (provider_type, raw_status)
  select p_provider_type, p_raw_status
  where coalesce(btrim(p_provider_type), '') <> ''
    and coalesce(btrim(p_raw_status), '') <> ''
  on conflict (provider_type, lower(raw_status)) do update
    set last_seen = now(),
        hit_count = unmatched_provider_statuses.hit_count + 1,
        resolved_at = null,
        resolved_status_id = null,
        resolved_by = null;
$$;

revoke all on function public.log_unmatched_provider_status(text, text) from public;
revoke all on function public.log_unmatched_provider_status(text, text) from anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.log_unmatched_provider_status(text, text) to service_role;

-- 4. owner-side resolve: map + retro-apply + close, atomically
create or replace function public.resolve_unmatched_provider_status(p_unmatched_id uuid, p_status_id uuid)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
as $$
declare
  v_row public.unmatched_provider_statuses%rowtype;
  v_master public.package_statuses%rowtype;
  v_conflict_label text;
  v_already_mapped boolean;
  v_parcels integer := 0;
  v_added boolean := false;
begin
  if not (select public.is_owner()) then
    raise exception 'resolve_unmatched_provider_status: owner only';
  end if;

  select * into v_row from public.unmatched_provider_statuses
   where id = p_unmatched_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'unmatched status not found';
  end if;
  if v_row.resolved_at is not null then
    raise exception 'unmatched status already resolved';
  end if;

  select * into v_master from public.package_statuses where id = p_status_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'target package status not found';
  end if;

  -- the raw string must not already belong to a DIFFERENT master: two claimants
  -- would let match_package_status pick by semantic priority, not owner intent
  select ps.label into v_conflict_label
  from public.package_statuses ps
  where ps.id <> p_status_id
    and exists (
      select 1
      from jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(ps.delivery_service_statuses, '[]'::jsonb)) as v(val)
      where lower(extensions.unaccent(v.val)) = lower(extensions.unaccent(v_row.raw_status))
    )
  limit 1;
  if v_conflict_label is not null then
    raise exception 'raw status "%" is already mapped to status "%" — attach it there, or remove that mapping first', v_row.raw_status, v_conflict_label;
  end if;

  select exists (
    select 1
    from jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(v_master.delivery_service_statuses, '[]'::jsonb)) as v(val)
    where lower(extensions.unaccent(v.val)) = lower(extensions.unaccent(v_row.raw_status))
  ) into v_already_mapped;

  if not v_already_mapped then
    update public.package_statuses
       set delivery_service_statuses = coalesce(delivery_service_statuses, '[]'::jsonb) || to_jsonb(v_row.raw_status)
     where id = p_status_id;
    v_added := true;
  end if;

  -- retro-apply: parcels still sitting on this raw status take the mapped master;
  -- the parcel->order sync triggers cascade the rest (date_livraison, unpaid, stock)
  update public.company_parcels cp
     set status_id = p_status_id,
         raw_provider_status = null,
         updated_at = now()
   where cp.raw_provider_status is not null
     and lower(extensions.unaccent(cp.raw_provider_status)) = lower(extensions.unaccent(v_row.raw_status))
     and cp.status_id is distinct from p_status_id;
  get diagnostics v_parcels = row_count;

  update public.unmatched_provider_statuses
     set resolved_at = now(),
         resolved_status_id = p_status_id,
         resolved_by = auth.uid()
   where id = p_unmatched_id;

  return jsonb_build_object('parcels_updated', v_parcels, 'mapping_added', v_added);
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.resolve_unmatched_provider_status(uuid, uuid) from public;
revoke all on function public.resolve_unmatched_provider_status(uuid, uuid) from anon;
grant execute on function public.resolve_unmatched_provider_status(uuid, uuid) to authenticated;
-- =====================================================================
-- Recurring charges: daily / monthly / yearly recurring expenses,
-- materialised into public.expenses on an OCCURRENCE basis (a real row
-- per due date). Analytics + Expenses list + audit work unchanged
-- because everything still reads public.expenses by date.
-- =====================================================================

-- 1. Definition table -------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.recurring_charges (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  "DigiCom Business" uuid not null references public.businesses(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null,
  type_id uuid references public.expense_types(id) on delete set null,
  amount numeric not null default 0 check (amount > 0),
  frequency text not null check (frequency in ('daily','monthly','yearly')),
  start_date date not null,
  end_date date,
  active boolean not null default true,
  description text,
  last_generated_date date,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint recurring_charges_date_range check (end_date is null or end_date >= start_date)
);

create index if not exists idx_recurring_charges_business on public.recurring_charges ("DigiCom Business");
create index if not exists idx_recurring_charges_active on public.recurring_charges (active) where active;

-- 2. Link materialised occurrences back to their definition -----------
alter table public.expenses
  add column if not exists recurring_id uuid references public.recurring_charges(id) on delete set null,
  add column if not exists occurrence_date date;

-- Idempotency: at most one expense per (recurring charge, occurrence day).
-- Manual rows keep both columns NULL; NULLs are DISTINCT in Postgres so
-- unlimited manual rows are allowed and never conflict.
create unique index if not exists ux_expenses_recurring_occurrence
  on public.expenses (recurring_id, occurrence_date);
create index if not exists idx_expenses_recurring_id
  on public.expenses (recurring_id) where recurring_id is not null;

-- 3. RLS (mirror expense_types: any authenticated user; business scoping
--    is enforced client-side via the "DigiCom Business" filter, same as
--    every other table in this app).
alter table public.recurring_charges enable row level security;

create policy "Authenticated users can view recurring_charges"
  on public.recurring_charges for select
  using ((select auth.role()) = 'authenticated');
create policy "Authenticated users can insert recurring_charges"
  on public.recurring_charges for insert
  with check ((select auth.role()) = 'authenticated');
create policy "Authenticated users can update recurring_charges"
  on public.recurring_charges for update
  using ((select auth.role()) = 'authenticated')
  with check ((select auth.role()) = 'authenticated');
create policy "Authenticated users can delete recurring_charges"
  on public.recurring_charges for delete
  using ((select auth.role()) = 'authenticated');

-- 4. updated_at maintenance ------------------------------------------
create or replace function public.tg_touch_updated_at()
returns trigger language plpgsql set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  new.updated_at = now();
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists trg_recurring_charges_touch on public.recurring_charges;
create trigger trg_recurring_charges_touch
  before update on public.recurring_charges
  for each row execute function public.tg_touch_updated_at();

-- 5. Generator --------------------------------------------------------
-- Materialise every due occurrence from start_date up to p_through
-- (default = today), bounded by end_date. Idempotent: re-running only
-- inserts occurrences that are missing. p_id null => all charges.
create or replace function public.generate_recurring_charges(
  p_through date default current_date,
  p_id uuid default null
)
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  r record;
  v_total integer := 0;
  v_rows integer;
  v_end date;
begin
  for r in
    select * from public.recurring_charges
    where active = true
      and start_date <= p_through
      and (p_id is null or id = p_id)
  loop
    v_end := least(p_through, coalesce(r.end_date, p_through));
    if v_end < r.start_date then
      continue;
    end if;

    if r.frequency = 'daily' then
      insert into public.expenses
        (name, amount, type_id, date, description, "DigiCom Business", recurring_id, occurrence_date)
      select r.name, r.amount, r.type_id,
             (x.d::text || ' 12:00:00+00')::timestamptz,
             r.description, r."DigiCom Business", r.id, x.d
-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- Partial payouts — sellers + mediabuyers
--
-- Pre-state: `seller_payments` and `mediabuyer_payments` are 1 row per
-- (collaborator, payout_month) with a binary `status='pending'|'paid'` and a
-- monolithic `total_amount`.
--
-- Post-state:
--   - A new audit-grade txn table per role (`*_payment_txns`) holds every
--     actual cash disbursement (amount, paid_at, note, created_by).
--   - The parent row keeps `total_amount` (what was earned) and gains
--     `paid_amount` (running sum of txns) maintained by triggers.
--   - `status` becomes a derived field still stored on the row for fast
--     filtering: 'pending' (0 paid), 'partial' (0 < paid < total), 'paid'
--     (paid >= total).
--   - Existing `status='paid'` rows are backfilled with a single synthetic txn
--     at their `total_amount`, preserving their `paid_at`. Anything reading
--     `status='paid'` keeps working — partial is a new value.
-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

ALTER TABLE public.seller_payments DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS seller_payments_status_check;
ALTER TABLE public.seller_payments
    ADD CONSTRAINT seller_payments_status_check CHECK (status IN ('pending','partial','paid'));

ALTER TABLE public.mediabuyer_payments DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS mediabuyer_payments_status_check;
ALTER TABLE public.mediabuyer_payments
    ADD CONSTRAINT mediabuyer_payments_status_check CHECK (status IN ('pending','partial','paid'));

ALTER TABLE public.seller_payments
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paid_amount numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE public.mediabuyer_payments
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paid_amount numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.seller_payment_txns (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    seller_payment_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.seller_payments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    amount numeric NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    paid_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    note text,
    created_by uuid,
    "DigiCom Business" uuid,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_seller_payment_txns_payment ON public.seller_payment_txns(seller_payment_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_seller_payment_txns_business ON public.seller_payment_txns("DigiCom Business");

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.mediabuyer_payment_txns (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    mediabuyer_payment_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.mediabuyer_payments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    amount numeric NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    paid_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    note text,
    created_by uuid,
    "DigiCom Business" uuid,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mediabuyer_payment_txns_payment ON public.mediabuyer_payment_txns(mediabuyer_payment_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mediabuyer_payment_txns_business ON public.mediabuyer_payment_txns("DigiCom Business");

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refresh_seller_payment_balance(p_payment_id uuid)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_paid numeric;
    v_total numeric;
    v_new_status text;
    v_paid_at timestamptz;
BEGIN
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) INTO v_paid
    FROM public.seller_payment_txns WHERE seller_payment_id = p_payment_id;

    SELECT total_amount INTO v_total
    FROM public.seller_payments WHERE id = p_payment_id;

    IF v_paid <= 0 THEN
        v_new_status := 'pending';
        v_paid_at := NULL;
    ELSIF v_paid >= COALESCE(v_total, 0) AND COALESCE(v_total, 0) > 0 THEN
        v_new_status := 'paid';
        SELECT MAX(paid_at) INTO v_paid_at FROM public.seller_payment_txns WHERE seller_payment_id = p_payment_id;
    ELSE
        v_new_status := 'partial';
        v_paid_at := NULL;
    END IF;

    UPDATE public.seller_payments
    SET paid_amount = v_paid,
        status      = v_new_status,
        paid_at     = v_paid_at
    WHERE id = p_payment_id;
END;
$$;

1. **HIGH — Ref collision can transfer a demand across tenants.** [migration:31](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/migrations/20260729100000_parcel_change_demands.sql:31>) and [provider-integration:4604](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts:4604>). `ref` is globally unique, and the service-role upsert updates ownership fields. If two Coliaty accounts return the same ref, the later request overwrites the first row’s business, order, provider, tracking code, and creator. Fix: make uniqueness/provider conflict scope `(provider_id, ref)` and never overwrite identity/tenant columns on duplicate.

2. **HIGH — Negated acceptance is permanently classified as approval.** [provider-webhook:242](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:242>). Values such as `NOT_ACCEPTED`, `NON_ACCEPTE`, or `REJECTED_NOT_ACCEPTED` match `/accept/` first, become `APPROVED`, and first-conclusion-wins prevents correction by a later rejection. Fix: recognize rejected and negated-acceptance forms before positive approval, using bounded status tokens.

3. **HIGH — Demand-only webhooks cannot reach reconciliation.** [provider-webhook:147](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:147>) and [provider-webhook:223](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:223>). A plausible payload shaped like `{EVENT, data:{package_code, details:{ref:"PCD-…",status:"APPROVED"}}}` has no recognized tracking field and returns at line 150. If `payload.ref` contains the PCD ref, it is misread as the tracking code and returns “Order not found” at line 173. Fix: run demand detection/reconciliation immediately after authenticated logging, before tracking/order-required exits.

4. **MEDIUM — Generic demand status can enter and corrupt the parcel-status pipeline.** [provider-webhook:294](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:294>). A demand event with `STATUS=APPROVED` reconciles the demand but counts as a normal status candidate, so it falls through and either changes the parcel/order if mapped or writes `APPROVED` into `raw_provider_status` and the unmatched-status queue. Fix: for an explicitly demand-named event, consume its verdict status and return unless a separate parcel-lifecycle field exists.

5. **MEDIUM — Demand cards are scoped only by tracking text.** [ParcelTrackingDialog.tsx:133](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/src/components/orders/ParcelTrackingDialog.tsx:133>). Tracking codes are carrier-scoped, but the query and cache key omit `order_id` and `provider_id`. Two parcels in the same business using different carriers but the same tracking string share cached demand data and render the wrong cards. Fix: key by `orderId` plus tracking code and query with `.eq('order_id', orderId)`; update invalidation to match.

6. **LOW — `DEMAND_STATUS` is discarded from the audit field.** [provider-webhook:252](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:252>). A payload whose only verdict field is `DEMAND_STATUS=APPROVED` updates the status but stores the event name or `null` as `provider_status_raw`. Fix: include `payload.DEMAND_STATUS` first in `providerStatusRaw`.

Material defects refute correctness.

Fix: run demand detection/reconciliation immediately after authenticated logging, before tracking/order-required exits.

4. **MEDIUM — Generic demand status can enter and corrupt the parcel-status pipeline.** [provider-webhook:294](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:294>). A demand event with `STATUS=APPROVED` reconciles the demand but counts as a normal status candidate, so it falls through and either changes the parcel/order if mapped or writes `APPROVED` into `raw_provider_status` and the unmatched-status queue. Fix: for an explicitly demand-named event, consume its verdict status and return unless a separate parcel-lifecycle field exists.

5. **MEDIUM — Demand cards are scoped only by tracking text.** [ParcelTrackingDialog.tsx:133](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/src/components/orders/ParcelTrackingDialog.tsx:133>). Tracking codes are carrier-scoped, but the query and cache key omit `order_id` and `provider_id`. Two parcels in the same business using different carriers but the same tracking string share cached demand data and render the wrong cards. Fix: key by `orderId` plus tracking code and query with `.eq('order_id', orderId)`; update invalidation to match.

6. **LOW — `DEMAND_STATUS` is discarded from the audit field.** [provider-webhook:252](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/provider-webhook/index.ts:252>). A payload whose only verdict field is `DEMAND_STATUS=APPROVED` updates the status but stores the event name or `null` as `provider_status_raw`. Fix: include `payload.DEMAND_STATUS` first in `providerStatusRaw`.

Material defects refute correctness.

Verified non-findings: tenant SELECT RLS has no direct policy escape; `.eq('DigiCom Business', businessId)` is valid and tenant-scoped; `package_city_label` is not forwarded to Coliaty; React keys do not collide; date rendering is guarded. Frontend TypeScript, both Deno checks, and `git diff --check` passed.
